+
+def _part_of(name):
+    if name in K.ACT_ARMS:
+        return 'act'
+    if name in B1_ARMS:
+        return 'standing-b1'
+    if name in B2_ARMS:
+        return 'standing-b2a'
+    if name in B3_ARMS:
+        return 'standing-b3'
+    if name in C_ARMS:
+        return 'standing-c'
+    return 'standing-a'
+
+
+def regenerate():
+    r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, '-X', 'utf8', os.path.join(T, 'terminal_table.py')], capture_output=True, text=True, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+    try:
+        diff = json.loads(rd('terminal_table_diff.json') or '{}')
+    except Exception:
+        diff = {}
+    return r.returncode, diff
+
+
+def _prev_verdicts(EX):
+    """(R325)(2)(iii): each arm's verdict on b714's banks beside b714's own (b714's post-push combine, data/b714_exercise.json `exercise`, its
+    `live` per arm): PASS ON PREV; FAILED AS b714 FAILED it; DIVERGE where b714 passed it and it fails here; NOT RUN BY b714 where b714 has no
+    row and it fails here (an arm new at b715 that cannot read b714's banks)."""
+    own = C3.jl('b714_exercise.json').get('exercise') or {}
+    out = dict(pass_on_prev=[], fail_as_prev=[], diverge=[], not_run_by_prev=[])
+    for n, e in EX.items():
+        if e['live']:
+            out['pass_on_prev'].append(n)
+        elif n not in own:
+            out['not_run_by_prev'].append(n)
+        elif (own[n] or {}).get('live') is False:
+            out['fail_as_prev'].append(n)
+        else:
+            out['diverge'].append(n)
+    return out
+
+
+# ### (R325)(4)(ii): the withheld field read from the ruling (data/act_current.json `withheld`, empty this act: G-RULE-LONG-PHASE-SPLIT sealed,
+# ### its input repaired by (4)(i)), each entry withheld only while its condition holds (tools/standing/close.py withheld_entries)
+WITHHELD = tuple(STC.withheld_entries(C3.jl('act_current.json').get('withheld') or []))
+
+
+def neg_shown():
+    """(R325)(2)(iv), standing clauses v2 row (55): the suite refuses to seal an arm whose NEG control -- the mutated copy, the POS column of a
+    part's table (its planted source; the arm must FAIL on it) -- has not been shown to fail, read from the pre-run's exercise banks
+    (data/b715_exercise_<part>.json, `mode` prerun, every part's). (R325)(4)(vi): by tools/standing/close.py neg_shown, its three lists read
+    by name (shown, bad, withheld), the withheld entries those of the ruling's field whose condition holds."""
+    banks = dict((p, C3.jl('b715_exercise_%s.json' % p)) for p in K.PARTS)
+    r = STC.neg_shown([(a[0], _part_of(a[0])) for a in ARMS], banks, list(WITHHELD))
+    return r.shown, r.bad, r.withheld
+
+
+def prev_run_missing():
+    """(R325)(2)(iii): the parts carrying arms whose run on b714's banks is not banked (data/b715_prevrun_exercise_<part>.json, `mode` prev)."""
+    return [p for p in sorted(set(_part_of(a[0]) for a in ARMS)) if C3.jl('b715_prevrun_exercise_%s.json' % p).get('mode') != 'prev']
+
+
+def seal():
+    face = read(FACE)
+    lock = utc_epoch(face, 'locked at (UTC)')
+    p = os.path.join(D, 'b715_seal_hashes.json')
+    if lock is None:
+        print('### THE FACE IS NOT LOCKED -- NOTHING RECORDED')
+        return 3
+    if os.path.exists(p):
+        print('### THE HASHES ARE RECORDED ALREADY -- NOTHING RECORDED TWICE')
+        return 3
+    shown, bad, withheld = neg_shown()
+    missing = prev_run_missing()
+    for n, why in withheld:
+        print('  WITHHELD, NOT EXERCISABLE: %-46s %s' % (n, why))
+    if bad or missing:
+        print('### (R325)(2)(iv): %d OF %d ARMS WITHOUT THEIR NEG CONTROL SHOWN FAILING ON A MUTATED COPY IN THE PRE-RUN:' % (len(bad), len(ARMS)))
+        for n, why in bad:
+            print('  %-46s %s' % (n, why))
+        print('### (R325)(2)(iii): THE PARTS WHOSE ARMS ARE NOT RUN ON b714`S BANKS BEFORE THE SEAL: %s' % (missing or 'NONE'))
+        print('### ### **THE SEAL REFUSED -- NOTHING RECORDED.**')
+        return 3
+    dtext = rd('b715_defects.txt')
+    now = sealed_now()
+    # ### (R325)(2)(i): the letters entered before the seal, recorded with it -- the trail's lines and the three trail-equality arms read these
+    j = dict(at=time.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ', time.gmtime()), lock=time.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ', time.gmtime(lock)), tools=now,
+             relay_head=gs(ROOT, 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'), letters=REC0.letters_of(dtext), defects_sha256=hashlib.sha256(dtext.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest(),
+             neg_shown=len(shown), withheld=[dict(arm=n, reason=why) for n, why in withheld], prev_run=dict((p_, dict((k_, len(v_)) for k_, v_ in C3.jl('b715_prevrun_exercise_%s.json' % p_).items()
+                                                          if k_ in ('pass_on_prev', 'fail_as_prev', 'diverge', 'not_run_by_prev')))
+                                                 for p_ in sorted(set(_part_of(a[0]) for a in ARMS))))
+    b = (json.dumps(j, indent=1, ensure_ascii=False) + NL).encode('utf-8')
+    _membrane(p)
+    with open(p + '.tmp', 'wb') as fh:
+        fh.write(b)
+    os.replace(p + '.tmp', p)
+    for t in K.SEALED:
+        print('  %-38s %s' % (t, now[t]))
+    print('  the letters entered before the seal %d: %s' % (len(j['letters']), ', '.join(s[:3] for s in j['letters']) or 'NONE'))
+    print('  the arms with their NEG control shown failing %d of %d ; withheld, not exercisable, %d (%s) ; the runs on b714`s banks %s' % (
+        len(shown), len(ARMS), len(withheld), ', '.join(n for n, _w in withheld) or 'NONE', j['prev_run']))
+    print('  written: b715_seal_hashes.json')
+    return 0
+
+
+def _base_name(pushed):
+    if PREV:
+        return 'b715_prevrun.txt'
+    if PRERUN:
+        return 'b715_arms_prerun.txt'
+    if MID and len(sys.argv) > sys.argv.index('--mid') + 1:
+        return sys.argv[sys.argv.index('--mid') + 1]
+    if RERUN and len(sys.argv) > sys.argv.index('--rerun-postpush') + 1:
+        return sys.argv[sys.argv.index('--rerun-postpush') + 1]
+    return 'b715_checks_postpush.txt' if pushed else 'b715_checks.txt'
+
+
+def _part_name(base, part):
+    return base.replace('.txt', '_%s.txt' % part)
+
+
+def _membrane(p):
+    """(R325)(2)(ii), standing clauses v2 row (53): the membrane before every bank this suite writes (tools/act_membrane.py guard_path, this act's
+    ROOT_EXCLUDE): the post-push parts' banks are excluded kinds (checks*, lsr*, exercise*, chain_*); the pre-run's, the run on b714's banks and
+    the seal's record are root items, refused once the root is appended."""
+    import act_membrane as MEM
+    MEM.guard_path(ACT_NOW, p, REC0.R.ROOT_EXCLUDE)
+
+
+def _write(name, lines):
+    out = os.path.join(D, name)
+    b = (NL.join(lines) + NL).encode('utf-8')
+    _membrane(out)
+    with open(out + '.tmp', 'wb') as fh:
+        fh.write(b)
+    os.replace(out + '.tmp', out)
+    return out
+
+
+def writer_plan(prerun, rerun, mid, pushed):
+    """the banks the writer block at the end of a part would write, by mode -- the part bank and the ls-remote bank in every mode, the exercise
+    bank in every mode, the per-part seconds bank under `if PRERUN:` alone. Read by `--guard-dry`; the writer block beneath follows this plan
+    exactly (the arm G-GUARD-HELD reads the block's own text)."""
+    base = 'b715_arms_prerun.txt' if prerun else ('b715_checks_postpush.txt' if (rerun or pushed) else 'b715_checks.txt')
+    out = [('data/' + _part_name(base, '<part>')), ('data/' + _part_name(base, '<part>').replace('b715_arms_prerun', 'b715_lsr_prerun').replace('b715_checks', 'b715_lsr').replace('.txt', '.json')),
+           'data/b715_exercise_<part>.json']
+    seconds = ['data/b715_arm_seconds_<part>.json'] if prerun else []
+    return out, seconds
+
+
+def guard_dry():
+    for mode, args in (('prerun', (True, False, False, False)), ('pre-push', (False, False, False, False)), ('post-push', (False, True, False, True))):
+        out, seconds = writer_plan(*args)
+        print('  MODE %-9s | the part`s banks: %s | the per-part seconds bank: %s' % (mode, ', '.join(out), ', '.join(seconds) or 'NONE'))
+    print('  ### POST-ROOT PATH WRITES NONE of the root-named per-part seconds banks: the pre-push and post-push modes write no data/b715_arm_seconds_<part>.json')
+    return 0
+
+
+def withheld_field():
+    """the author's word (7): the arms in the seal record's withheld field (data/b715_seal_hashes.json `withheld`, written by seal()); before
+    the seal, none. Every tally the suite writes after the seal reads it."""
+    return [w.get('arm') for w in (C3.jl('b715_seal_hashes.json').get('withheld') or [])]
+
+
+def _tally(EX, W):
+    """the author's word (7): a part's figures from its exercise rows -- an arm in the withheld field W counted neither among the passes nor
+    among the live failures (nor the controls' figures); every other arm as before. Returns (live failing, positive-control passes,
+    negative-control failures, withheld)."""
+    fail = [n for n, e in EX.items() if n not in W and not e['live']]
+    defective = [n for n, e in EX.items() if n not in W and e['pos']]
+    negfail = sum(1 for n, e in EX.items() if n not in W and e['neg'] != e['live'])
+    return fail, defective, negfail, [n for n in EX if n in W]
+
+
+ARM_ROW = re.compile(r'^  (G-[\w-]+)\s+(PASS|FAIL)\s+(PASS|FAIL)\s+(PASS|FAIL)\s+[\d.]+\s+(.*)$', re.M)
+
+
+def _combine_tally(parts, W):
+    """the author's word (7): the combine's figures summed from the parts' footers, an arm in the withheld field W taken out by its row -- a
+    part written before the field (the pre-run's) counted it as it fell, so its row's LIVE, NEG and POS are taken off the sums; a part written
+    after prints the row WITHHELD and its footer left it out. Returns (arms, passing, failing, neg failures, pos passes, failing names,
+    pos-pass names, withheld names). By tools/standing/close.py combine_tally (the coverage table's [7], [17], [20]; replayed EQUAL)."""
+    return STC.combine_tally(parts, W)
+
+
+def combine():
+    base = sys.argv[sys.argv.index('--combine') + 1]
+    parts, heads = [], []
+    for part in K.PARTS:
+        t = rd(_part_name(base, part))
+        m = PART_FOOT.search(t)
+        if not m or m.group(1) != part:
+            print('### THE PART BANK %s HAS NO FOOTER -- NOTHING WRITTEN' % _part_name(base, part))
+            return 2
+        parts.append((part, m, t))
+        heads.append(t.split(NL)[1] if len(t.split(NL)) > 1 else '')
+    n, lp, lf, ng, pc, fails, defs, wh = _combine_tally(parts, withheld_field())
+    deq = all(m.group(9) == 'True' for _p, m, _t in parts)
+    carried = [f for f in fails if f in CARRIED_FAILING]
+    ok = lf == len(carried) and pc == 0 and ng == 0 and deq
+    out = ['=' * 104, heads[0].replace('PART standing-a', 'SEVEN PARTS SUMMED'), '=' * 104] + [
+        '  ### PART %s : ARMS %s ; %s s' % (p, m.group(2), m.group(10)) for p, m, _t in parts] + [
+        '  ### the parts` seconds summed %d ; the longest part %s s, under the 590 s guard %s' % (
+            sum(int(m.group(10)) for _p, m, _t in parts), max(int(m.group(10)) for _p, m, _t in parts),
+            max(int(m.group(10)) for _p, m, _t in parts) < 590),
+        '  ### the failures declared on the face as carried (section (G2)): %s ; failing among them %s ; failing beyond them %s' % (
+            list(CARRIED_FAILING) or 'NONE', carried or 'NONE', [f for f in fails if f not in CARRIED_FAILING] or 'NONE'),
+        '  ### ### **ARMS RUN : %d. ### LIVE PASSING : %d. ### LIVE FAILING : %d %s. ### WITHHELD, NOT EXERCISABLE : %d %s.**' % (
+            n, lp, lf, fails or '', len(wh), wh or ''),
+        '  ### ### **NEGATIVE-CONTROL FAILURES : %d. ### POSITIVE-CONTROL PASSES : %d %s.**' % (ng, pc, defs or ''),
+        '  ### ### **VERDICT : %s%s**' % ('ALL ARMS PASS AND EVERY CONTROL BEHAVES' if (ok and not fails) else
+                                         'EVERY ARM PASSES BUT THE CARRIED FAILURE DECLARED ON THE FACE, AND EVERY CONTROL BEHAVES' if ok else 'NOT CLEAN',
+                                         ' ; WITHHELD, NOT EXERCISABLE, COUNTED NEITHER PASSING NOR FAILING: %s' % ', '.join(wh) if wh else ''),
+        '=' * 104, '']
+    for p, _m, t in parts:
+        out += ['### THE PART BANK %s, WHOLE:' % _part_name(base, p)] + t.rstrip(NL).split(NL) + ['']
+    _write(base, out)
+    ex = {}
+    for part in K.PARTS:
+        ex.update(jl('b715_exercise_%s.json' % part).get('exercise') or {})
+    if ex and base in ('b715_checks.txt', 'b715_checks_postpush.txt'):
+        e = (json.dumps(dict(exercise=ex, run=n, live_failing=fails, defective=defs, neg_failures=ng, withheld=wh), indent=1, ensure_ascii=False) + NL).encode('utf-8')
+        p_ = os.path.join(D, 'b715_exercise.json')
+        _membrane(p_)
+        with open(p_ + '.tmp', 'wb') as fh:
+            fh.write(e)
+        os.replace(p_ + '.tmp', p_)
+    print(NL.join(out[3:16]))
+    print('  written: %s' % base)
+    return 0 if ok else 1
+
+
+# ### the carried failures declared on the face by name (section (0)): G-READ-RULE-STANDING failing on the Read calls the read bank counts
+# ### (relay data/b715_read_calls.txt), defects (i) and (k) among them; G-PRIORBANK-UNCHANGED failing on data/b714_steps.json, b714's closing
+# ### step row committed alone at 3a41b584 by (R325)(11)'s order; G-CORRESPONDENCE-CURRENCY failing on the figure 1 against b714's 0, the tag
+# ### v0.14.0 moving THE_UNCONDITIONAL_SURROUND :183's lv row (v0.13.0 -> v0.14.0, T3_perClass_to_combinations; records (9)). The combine reads
+# ### them by name, and no other failure is carried.
+CARRIED_FAILING = ('G-READ-RULE-STANDING', 'G-PRIORBANK-UNCHANGED')   # ### b715's two, read off the mid-act standing-c run (b714's third, G-CORRESPONDENCE-CURRENCY, passing)
+
+
+def main():
+    if SEAL:
+        return seal()
+    if COMBINE:
+        return combine()
+    if GUARD_DRY:
+        return guard_dry()
+    if PART not in K.PARTS:
+        print('### every run in seven foreground parts -- give `--part %s`' % ' | '.join(K.PARTS))
+        return 2
+    fm = free_mb()
+    if fm < K.HOLD:
+        print('%s  ### THE HOST`S FREE MEMORY %d MB, BENEATH THE HOLD %d MB: PART %s NOT RUN ((R294)(2))' % (
+            time.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ', time.gmtime()), fm, K.HOLD, PART))
+        return 3
+    t_start = time.time()
+    pushed = RERUN or (not PRERUN and not MID and not PREV and is_pushed())
+    base = _base_name(pushed)
+    rec('=' * 104)
+    rec('b715 -- THE SUITE. ### **%s READING.** ### PART %s ### every arm exercised on both controls.' % (
+        'ON b714`S BANKS ((R325)(2)(iii))' if PREV else 'PRE-SEAL (R202)(3)' if PRERUN else 'MID-ACT' if MID else 'POST-PUSH' if pushed else 'PRE-PUSH', PART))
+    rec('### the host`s free memory at the part`s start %d MB (the hold %d MB)' % (fm, K.HOLD))
+    if PRERUN or MID:
+        rec('### run at (UTC) : %s   ### %s' % (time.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ', time.gmtime()),
+                                              'the standing line of (R202)(3): every arm run at HEAD before the face is sealed, its count printed.'
+                                              if PRERUN else 'a mid-act re-run; no table regenerated.'))
+    rec('=' * 104)
+    S = sources(NEED[PART])
+    S['pushed'] = pushed
+    if PART == 'standing-b3':
+        jh = _JL_OWN or jl
+        a1, a2 = jh(half_bank('a1', _run_key(base))), jh(half_bank('a2', _run_key(base)))
+        S['half_a'] = dict(head=a1.get('head') if a1.get('head') == a2.get('head') else None, acts=(a1.get('acts') or []) + (a2.get('acts') or []),
+                           reads=dict(a1.get('reads') or {}, **(a2.get('reads') or {})), boundary=a2.get('boundary') or a1.get('boundary') or {})
+    t1 = time.time()
+    regen = PART == 'act' and not (RERUN or PRERUN or MID or PREV)
+    rc_gen, gen_diff = regenerate() if regen else (0, dict(rerun=True))
+    S['phase_secs']['the table regenerated' + ('' if regen else ' (not in this part or run)')] = time.time() - t1
+    rec('  ### THE PHASES, IN SECONDS: %s' % '; '.join('%s %.0f' % kv for kv in S['phase_secs'].items()))
+    rec('  ### THE LONGEST PHASE %.0f s ; above 450 s: %s (b675 rules row 2)' % (max(S['phase_secs'].values()), max(S['phase_secs'].values()) > 450))
+    declared = g2_names(S['face'])
+    names = [a[0] for a in ARMS]
+    S['declared_eq_run'] = sorted(names) == declared and len(names) == len(set(names))
+    arms = [a for a in ARMS if _part_of(a[0]) == PART]
+    rec('  arms in the (G2) block : %d ; in the suite : %d ; run in this part : %d' % (len(declared), len(names), len(arms)))
+    if set(names) != set(declared):
+        rec('  ### declared not run : %s' % sorted(set(declared) - set(names)))
+        rec('  ### run not declared : %s' % sorted(set(names) - set(declared)))
+    rec('  ### (R264)(2) THE FREEZE: the standing arms (b714`s face less its own arms, the twenty-four rule arms and G-READ-RULE-STANDING among them; none added) %d ; '
+        'carried here %d ; the act`s arms over its own banks %s ; arms beyond both %s' % (
+            len(S['standing']), len(set(names) & set(S['standing'])), sorted(set(names) - set(S['standing'])),
+            sorted(set(names) - set(S['standing']) - set(K.ACT_ARMS)) or 'NONE'))
+    rec('  ### standing arms not carried here: %s' % (sorted(set(S['standing']) - set(names)) or 'NONE'))
+    if 'figures' in NEED[PART]:
+        rec('  ### THE FOUR STANDING FIGURES, each against the previous act`s (%s):' % S['fig_prev_name'])
+        for k in ('correspondence', 'readme', 'partners', 'pins'):
+            rec('  ###   %-15s live %s ; previous %s' % (k, (S['fig'].get(k) or {}).get('figure'), (S['fig_prev'].get(k) or {}).get('figure')))
+        ok_, n_, off_, pre_, form_ = RDA.reading_text(S['reads_bank'])
+        rec('  ### G-READ-RULE-STANDING`S READING (relay data/b715_read_calls.txt): %s ; rows %d ; offences %d %s ; the cap`s precursors %d' % (
+            'PASS' if ok_ else 'FAIL', n_, len(off_), [RDA._show(r) for r in off_], len(pre_)))
+    if 'rules' in NEED[PART]:
+        rec('  ### THE RULE ARMS, EACH WITH ITS COUNT (tools/rule_arms.py; rows read, rows failing, the reading); the call trace %d calls:' % len(
+            S['ra'].get('calls') or []))
+        for nm in RA.NAMES:
+            ok_, n_, b_, note = RA.reading(nm, S['ra'])
+            rec('  ###   %-28s %-4s read %d ; failing %d ; %s' % (nm, 'PASS' if ok_ else 'FAIL', n_, b_, note[:260]))
+    chain_part = next((x for x in ('chain_a1', 'chain_a2', 'chain_b') if x in NEED[PART]), None)
+    if chain_part:
+        key, span = {'chain_a1': ('a1', K.CHAIN_QUARTERS[0]), 'chain_a2': ('a2', K.CHAIN_QUARTERS[1]), 'chain_b': ('b', K.CHAIN_HALVES[1])}[chain_part]
+        av = S['ar_verify']
+        bd = S['boundary']
+        rec('  ### THE ACT-ROOT CHAIN`S PART %s, %s-%s, recomputed at commit: %s' % (key.upper(), span[0], span[1],
+                                                                                 '; '.join('%s %s%s' % (a, v, (' (' + '; '.join(w) + ')') if w else '') for a, v, w in av) or 'no act rooted'))
+        rec('  ### THE BOUNDARY (relay data/act_roots.txt): %s root %s ; %s previous %s ; equal %s' % (bd['first'], bd['root'], bd['second'], bd['previous'], bd['equal']))
+        if key != 'b':
+            rec('  ### the reads at commit, %s: %s' % (span[1], S['ar_reads'].get(span[1])))
+            rec('  ### PART %s CARRIES NO ARM: its verdicts banked for standing-b3 in relay data/%s' % (PART, half_bank(key, _run_key(base))))
+        else:
+            ha = S['half_a'] or {}
+            rec('  ### the reads at commit, b680: %s ; %s: %s' % (S['ar_reads'].get('b680'), ACT_NOW, S['ar_reads'].get(ACT_NOW)))
+            rec('  ### THE FIRST HALF AS standing-b2a AND standing-b2b BANKED IT (relay data/%s, data/%s): %d acts, AGREE %d, at HEAD %s (now %s), its boundary equal %s' % (
+                half_bank('a1', _run_key(base)), half_bank('a2', _run_key(base)), len(ha.get('acts') or []), sum(1 for x in ha.get('acts') or [] if x[1] == 'AGREE'),
+                (ha.get('head') or '-')[:12], gs(ROOT, 'rev-parse', 'HEAD')[:12], (ha.get('boundary') or {}).get('equal')))
+        write_half(key, _run_key(base), av, S['ar_reads'], span)
+    sj = S['sealj'].get('tools') or {}
+    rec('  ### THE SEALED TOOLS, recorded at the seal %s : %s' % (S['sealj'].get('at', '### NOT RECORDED'), '; '.join(
+        '%s %s' % (t, ('agree' if sj.get(t) == S['sealnow'].get(t) else 'DIFFER') if t in sj else 'not recorded') for t in K.SEALED)))
+    off = S['REC'].edit_route_offences(S['cmds'] + S['prev_cmds'])
+    rec('  ### G-EDIT-ROUTE read %d captured commands of this act and %d of the previous act after its post-push suite; offences %d: %s' % (
+        len(S['cmds']), len(S['prev_cmds']), len(off), ['#%s %s' % (r['n'], [k for k, _ in o]) for r, o in off] or 'none'))
+    mc = re.search(r'(?<!ANSWER )CITES CHECKED \d+ ; OK \d+ ; MISSES \d+', S['citation'])
+    rec('  ### THE CITATION CHECK: %s' % (mc.group(0) if mc else '### NOT READ'))
+    rec('  ### PLACE-papers` changed files on main %d against the allowed %d (FINDINGS, OPEN_TRAILS and the merged keystone): beyond %s' % (
+        len(S['pp_changed']), len(S['pp_allowed']), sorted(set(S['pp_changed']) - set(S['pp_allowed'])) or 'NONE'))
+    rec('  ### SIDE-explicit-formula: main %s ; v0.37 %s (unmoved) ; SIDE-lv-conservation main %s, v0.10.0 %s, port-b707 %s (b714`s tip, kept, no commit this act) ; the join kernel main %s ; runner dispatches this act %d (N1: none)' % (
+        S['ef_main'][:12], (S['ef_tags'].get('v0.37') or '')[:12], S['lv_main'][:12], (S['lv_v0100'] or '-')[:12], (S['lv_port'] or '-')[:12],
+        (S['join_main'] or '-')[:12], len(S['cmdj'].get('dispatches') or []) if S['cmdj'] else -1))
+    if 'live' in NEED[PART]:
+        lv = S['live']
+        rec('  ### THE THREE ARMS AT HEAD (seconds %s):' % ', '.join('%.0f' % x for x in lv['secs']))
+        rec('  ###   G-NO-SORRY-MAIN read %d kernels` mains, pairs unequal %d; sorry in code %d: %s' % (
+            len(lv['nsm']), sum(1 for r in lv['nsm'] if not r['equal']), len(nsm_hits(S)), '; '.join('%s %s:%d' % h for h in nsm_hits(S)) or 'none'))
+        rec('  ###   G-AXIOM-CLAIM read %d claims: CONTRADICTED %d, UNVERIFIABLE %d, VERIFIED %d, UNBOUND %d, MENTION %d' % (
+            sum(1 for r in lv['ac'] if r['verdict'] != 'OUT OF SCOPE'), ac_count(lv['ac'], 'CONTRADICTED'), ac_count(lv['ac'], 'UNVERIFIABLE'),
+            ac_count(lv['ac'], 'VERIFIED'), ac_count(lv['ac'], 'UNBOUND'), ac_count(lv['ac'], 'MENTION')))
+        rec('  ###   G-MODULE-HEADER read %d headers: OVERREACHES %d rows %s' % (sum(v['headers'] for v in lv['mh_per'].values()), len(lv['mh']),
+                                                                             ['%s %s:%s' % (x.get('kernel'), x.get('file'), x.get('line')) for x in lv['mh']][:6] or ''))
+    rec('  %-46s %-5s %-5s %-5s %-8s %s' % ('arm', 'LIVE', 'NEG', 'POS', 'secs', 'verdict'))
+    rec('  ' + '-' * 106)
+    W = withheld_field()
+    fail, defective, negfail, EX, SECS = [], [], 0, {}, {}
+    for name, reads, pred, pos in arms:
+        if name == 'G-ARMS-NO-LIVE-LIMB':
+            S['no_live_limb'] = not defective
+        ta = time.time()
+        try:
+            live = bool(pred(S))
+        except Exception as e:
+            live = False
+            rec('  ### %s raised %s: %s' % (name, type(e).__name__, str(e)[:160]))
+        try:
+            neg = bool(pred(copy.copy(S)))
+        except Exception:
+            neg = False
+        pos_raised = None   # ### (R325)(2)(iv): a plant or a predicate that raised on the mutated copy has not SHOWN the control failing
+        try:
+            m = pos(copy.copy(S))
+            if isinstance(m, dict):
+                posv = bool(pred(m))
+            else:
+                posv, pos_raised = False, 'the plant returned no source'
+        except Exception as e:
+            posv, pos_raised = False, '%s: %s' % (type(e).__name__, str(e)[:120])
+        SECS[name] = round(time.time() - ta, 4)
+        if not live:
+            fail.append(name)
+        if neg != live:
+            negfail += 1
+        if posv:
+            defective.append(name)
+        EX[name] = dict(live=live, neg=neg, pos=posv, pos_raised=pos_raised, shown_failing=(not posv and pos_raised is None), reads=reads, secs=SECS[name])
+        rec('  %-46s %-5s %-5s %-5s %-8.3f %s' % (name, 'PASS' if live else 'FAIL', 'PASS' if neg else 'FAIL', 'PASS' if posv else 'FAIL', SECS[name],
+                                                 'WITHHELD, NOT EXERCISABLE' if name in W else
+                                                 'OK' if (live and neg and not posv) else ('### POS PASSES -- DEFECTIVE' if posv else
+                                                                                           '### FAILS -- THE CARRIED FAILURE DECLARED ON THE FACE' if name in CARRIED_FAILING else '### FAILS')))
+    if not arms:
+        rec('  (no arm in this part)')
+    fail, defective, negfail, wh = _tally(EX, W)
+    rec('')
+    if 'written' in NEED[PART]:
+        rec('  ### tracked files written (%d), each against the (W) globs: uncovered %s' % (len(S['written']), [f for f in S['written']
+                                                                                              if not any(fnmatch.fnmatch(f, p) for p in S['globs'])] or 'NONE'))
+        rec('  ### G-PRIORBANK-UNCHANGED checked %d relay data banks tracked at %s by blob id; changed %s' % (S['prior_n'], PRE['relay'], S['prior_bad'] or 'NONE'))
+    lsr = dict(KC0.LSR)
+    rec('  ### OPEN_TRAILS :12703: ls-remote calls this part, per repository: %d repositories, at most %d each' % (len(lsr), max(lsr.values()) if lsr else 0))
+    if regen:
+        rec('  ### ### **(R107): THE GENERATOR WAS RE-RUN BY THIS SUITE.** ### exit %d.' % rc_gen)
+        rec('  ###   rows added %d ; rows gone %d ; grade-or-profile changed %d %s' % (len(gen_diff.get('added') or []), len(gen_diff.get('gone') or []),
+                                                                                 len(gen_diff.get('changed') or []), (gen_diff.get('changed') or '')[:20]))
+    pv = _prev_verdicts(EX) if PREV else {}
+    if PREV:
+        rec('  ### (R325)(2)(iii) THE ARMS ON b714`S BANKS, beside b714`s own verdicts (relay data/b714_exercise.json, b714`s post-push combine):')
+        for k_ in ('pass_on_prev', 'fail_as_prev', 'diverge', 'not_run_by_prev'):
+            rec('  ###   %-16s %d %s' % (k_.upper(), len(pv[k_]), pv[k_] or ''))
+        rec('  ### ### **ON b714`S BANKS: PASS %d ; FAILED AS b714 FAILED %d ; DIVERGE %d (b714 passed, here failed) ; NOT RUN BY b714 %d (here failed).**' % (
+            len(pv['pass_on_prev']), len(pv['fail_as_prev']), len(pv['diverge']), len(pv['not_run_by_prev'])))
+    secs = time.time() - t_start
+    rec('  ### the arms` own seconds summed %.1f, the slowest %s' % (sum(SECS.values()), ', '.join('%s %.2f' % kv for kv in sorted(SECS.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1])[:3]) or 'NONE'))
+    rec('  ### the run took %.0f s' % secs)
+    rec('  ### PART %s : ARMS %d ; LIVE PASSING %d ; LIVE FAILING %d %s ; NEGATIVE-CONTROL FAILURES %d ; POSITIVE-CONTROL PASSES %d %s ; '
+        'DECLARED EQ RUN %s ; %d s ; WITHHELD, NOT EXERCISABLE %d %s' % (PART, len(arms), len(arms) - len(fail) - len(wh), len(fail), json.dumps(fail) if fail else 'NONE',
+                                                                      negfail, len(defective), json.dumps(defective) if defective else 'NONE', S['declared_eq_run'],
+                                                                      int(secs), len(wh), json.dumps(wh) if wh else 'NONE'))
+    rec('=' * 104)
+    out = _write(_part_name(base, PART), L)
+    lp = os.path.join(D, _part_name(base, PART).replace('b715_arms_prerun', 'b715_lsr_prerun').replace('b715_checks', 'b715_lsr').replace('.txt', '.json'))
+    lb = (json.dumps(dict(run=os.path.basename(out), lsr=lsr), indent=1, ensure_ascii=False) + NL).encode('utf-8')
+    _membrane(lp)
+    with open(lp + '.tmp', 'wb') as fh:
+        fh.write(lb)
+    os.replace(lp + '.tmp', lp)
+    ex = dict(exercise=EX, run=len(arms), live_failing=fail, defective=defective, neg_failures=negfail, withheld=wh, part=PART,
+              mode='prev' if PREV else 'prerun' if PRERUN else 'mid' if MID else 'post-push' if pushed else 'pre-push',
+              at=time.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ', time.gmtime()), relay_head=gs(ROOT, 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'))
+    ex.update(pv)
+    ep = os.path.join(D, ('b715_prevrun_exercise_%s.json' if PREV else 'b715_exercise_%s.json') % PART)
+    _membrane(ep)
+    with open(ep + '.tmp', 'wb') as fh:
+        fh.write((json.dumps(ex, indent=1, ensure_ascii=False) + NL).encode('utf-8'))
+    os.replace(ep + '.tmp', ep)
+    # ### THE GUARD HELD -- the per-part seconds bank (root-named, gathered by the record tool's armsecs before the seal) is written by the pre-run
+    # ### alone, as tools/b700_checks.py :1669 had it and b702 restored; the pre-push and post-push runs write no root-named bank
+    if PRERUN:
+        sp = os.path.join(D, 'b715_arm_seconds_%s.json' % PART)
+        _membrane(sp)
+        with open(sp + '.tmp', 'wb') as fh:
+            fh.write((json.dumps(dict(at=time.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ', time.gmtime()), part=PART, arms=SECS, phases=S['phase_secs'], run_secs=int(secs)),
+                                 indent=1, ensure_ascii=False) + NL).encode('utf-8'))
+        os.replace(sp + '.tmp', sp)
+    print('  written: %s' % os.path.basename(out))
+    if PREV:
+        return 0 if not (pv['diverge'] or pv['not_run_by_prev']) else 1
+    carried_only =[f for f in fail if f not in CARRIED_FAILING] == [] and not defective and negfail == 0
+    return 0 if (not fail and not defective and negfail == 0) else (4 if carried_only else 1)
+
+
+class _PrevWorklist(object):
+    """(R325)(2)(iii): the worklist a run on b714's banks reads -- b714's value for every name b714's worklist carries, this act's for the suite's
+    own structure (the arms by part, the parts, the hold, the chain's halves) and for a name b714's lacks."""
+    OWN = ('ACT_ARMS', 'PARTS', 'HOLD', 'CHAIN_HALVES', 'CHAIN_QUARTERS', 'STANDING_ADDED')
+
+    def __init__(self, own, prev):
+        self._own, self._prev = own, prev
+
+    def __getattr__(self, n):
+        if n not in self.OWN and hasattr(self._prev, n):
+            return getattr(self._prev, n)
+        return getattr(self._own, n)
+
+
+_JL_OWN = None   # defect (j): the chain halves are this run's own banks, read past the rebinding
+
+
+def _prev_rebind():
+    """(R325)(2)(iii), `--prev`: every bank the arms read through rd and jl under this act's prefix read as b714's (data/b715_<x> as
+    data/b714_<x>), the face b714's, the worklist through _PrevWorklist, the pre-act heads b714's. The tools stay this act's; the banks alone are
+    the previous act's. An arm whose source the rebinding does not reach (a literal path, a b715-only bank) reads this act's and is printed as it
+    falls, beside b714's own verdict."""
+    global rd, jl, FACE, K, PRE, _JL_OWN
+
+    def pv(n):
+        return ('b714_' + n[5:]) if isinstance(n, str) and n.startswith('b715_') else n
+
+    rd0, jl0 = rd, jl
+    _JL_OWN = jl0
+    rd = lambda n: rd0(pv(n))   # noqa: E731
+    jl = lambda n: jl0(pv(n))   # noqa: E731
+    FACE = B707_FACE
+    K = _PrevWorklist(K, K707)
+    PRE = dict(relay=K707.PRE_RELAY, pp=K707.PRE_PP)
+
+
+if PREV:
+    _prev_rebind()
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    sys.exit(main())

+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""b715_closing.py -- THE CLOSING RECORD OF b715, UNDER (R325). Carried from b714_closing.py by tools/b715_carry.py (the act names substituted,
+data/b714_rules.txt row (1)), its act sections written for b715 by the Edit tool: the remote reads stored and looked up under ONE key (repo_key(),

+the tag rows reading a lightweight tag at its own line; each bank's verdict line and not its title; the citation line from the banks; the captures
+in b673's form; the defects row read from the bank (b693's repair); the head line's prompt count the answers bank's rows with nothing added and
+its scores from the scores-close bank ((R325)(3)(iii), replacing b700's repair (9)), its defect count the defects bank's lettered rows read now;
+the mirror line's sentence on the builder's state file read from its blob against its last commit ((R325)(3)(v)). The act's sections (Component 6, by the Edit
+tool) are the corrigendum, the standing toolset (the measurement, the scan, the replay, the coverage table), the class repairs and the hook, the
+roster from main and the pins census, the chain's census rows and the standing arms; the author's word after the seal printed from its bank

+order (KERNEL, CERTIFIED, REGISTERED, HELD) from the bank the unsealed report tool writes before the root, its scores line printed after the root
+by the tool's scores route and written nowhere. No kernel written this act: SIDE-lv-conservation's main, deposit, port branch and b714's tag
+v0.14.0, SIDE-faces-join and SIDE-explicit-formula read unmoved.

+written to the scratchpad and its readings printed; the censuses run into the scratchpad; no bank of relay data/ written.

+def run(args):
+    r = subprocess.run(args, capture_output=True, text=True, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace',
+                       env=dict(os.environ, PYTHONIOENCODING='utf-8'))
+    return r.returncode, (r.stdout or '') + (r.stderr or '')
+
+
+def g(repo, *a):
+    return run(['git', '-C', repo] + list(a))[1].strip()
+
+
+def rd(n):
+    p = os.path.join(D, n)
+    return io.open(p, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').read().replace(chr(13), '') if os.path.exists(p) else ''
+
+
+def jl(n):
+    try:
+        return json.load(io.open(os.path.join(D, n), encoding='utf-8'))
+    except Exception:
+        return {}
+
+
+def tags_now():
+    """the fixed list, b714's tag SIDE-lv-conservation v0.14.0 at port-b707's tip among them; no tag made this act; no kernel written; no main moved."""
+    return TAGS_FIXED
+
+
+VLINE = re.compile(r'^\s*### ### \*\*(.*?)\*\*\s*$', re.M)
+
+
+def vline(text):
+    m = VLINE.findall(text or '')
+    return m[-1].rstrip('.') if m else '### NO VERDICT LINE'
+
+
+def vtail(text):
+    m = re.findall(r'(?ms)^### ### \*\*(.*?)\*\*\s*$', text or '')
+    return ' '.join(m[-1].replace('###', ' ').split()).rstrip('.') if m else '### NO VERDICT LINE'
+
+
+def verdict(text, pat):
+    m = re.search(pat, text)
+    return m.group(0) if m else '### NOT FOUND'
+
+
+# ================================================================================ THE CARRIED-FORWARD DEFECTS ROW (b693's repair, carried)
+def _span(letters):
+    runs, cur = [], []
+    for x in letters:
+        if cur and len(x) == 1 and len(cur[-1]) == 1 and ord(x) == ord(cur[-1]) + 1:
+            cur.append(x)
+        else:
+            if cur:
+                runs.append(cur)
+            cur = [x]
+    if cur:
+        runs.append(cur)
+    return ', '.join('(%s)' % r[0] if len(r) == 1 else '(%s)–(%s)' % (r[0], r[-1]) for r in runs)
+
+
+DEFECT_ROW = re.compile(r"^    \(([a-z]'?)\) ")
+
+
+def defects_row(act, item='i'):
+    L = rd('%s_defects.txt' % act).split(NL)
+    rows = [(DEFECT_ROW.match(l).group(1), l) for l in L if DEFECT_ROW.match(l)]
+    if not rows:
+        return '    (%s) The defects: ### NO LETTERED ROW IN relay data/%s_defects.txt.' % (item, act)
+    ruled = [x for x, l in rows if 'A RULED EXCEPTION' in l]
+    nav = [x for x, l in rows if re.match(r"^    \([a-z]'?\) THE NAVIGATOR'S", l)]
+    tail = []
+    if ruled:
+        tail.append('%s the ruled exception%s' % (_span(ruled), '' if len(ruled) == 1 else 's'))
+    if nav:
+        tail.append('%s the navigator`s for the author`s strike' % _span(nav))
+    return '    (%s) The defects %s for the next ruling%s.' % (item, _span([x for x, _l in rows]), (', ' + ' and '.join(tail)) if tail else '')
+
+
+PILOT_KEYSTONE = re.compile(r'^  ### (day1/\S+) -- its Correspondence')
+PILOT_NOPIN = re.compile(r'^    :(\d+) +(\S+) +\| NO PIN STATED')
+
+
+def nopin_rows(text=None):
+    """the author's word (4)(3): the names the pilot read with NO PIN STATED (relay data/b715_pilot.txt (P1)), each by keystone, file and line:
+    [(keystone, line, name)], in the bank's order."""
+    out, cur = [], None
+    for l in (rd('%s_pilot.txt' % ACT) if text is None else text).split(NL):
+        m = PILOT_KEYSTONE.match(l)
+        if m:
+            cur = m.group(1)
+            continue
+        n = PILOT_NOPIN.match(l)
+        if n and cur:
+            out.append((cur, int(n.group(1)), n.group(2)))
+    return out
+
+
+def carried():
+    """the carried items: (R325)(8)'s for b716 read from b714's banks by name; the 19 NO PIN STATED names from b714's pilot bank (relay
+    data/b714_pilot.txt (P1)) by keystone, file and line."""
+    rows = nopin_rows(rd('b714_pilot.txt'))
+    by = []
+    for k in dict.fromkeys(r[0] for r in rows):
+        by.append('%s %s' % (k, ', '.join(':%d `%s`' % (n, x) for kk, n, x in rows if kk == k)))
+    pin = ['    (i) The %d names with NO PIN STATED (relay data/b714_pilot.txt (P1); (R325)(8)), for b716: each Correspondence row gains its' % len(rows),
+           '        repository and pin, read from the census at its current edition on main -- ' + '; '.join(by) + '.']
+    return CARRIED_FIXED[:-1] + [defects_row(ACT, 'g')] + CARRIED_FIXED[-1:] + pin
+
+
+# ================================================================================ THE REMOTE READS, ONE KEY (b694's repair, carried)
+_REMOTE = {}
+REREAD = []
+
+
+def repo_key(p):
+    s = str(p).replace('\\', '/')
+    m = re.match(r'^/([a-zA-Z])/(.*)$', s)
+    if m:
+        s = '%s:/%s' % (m.group(1), m.group(2))
+    m = re.match(r'^([a-zA-Z]):/*(.*)$', s)
+    if m:
+        s = '%s:/%s' % (m.group(1).upper(), m.group(2))
+    return s.rstrip('/')
+
+
+def _ls(p):
+    out = g(p, 'ls-remote', 'origin')
+    return dict((l.split('\t')[1].strip(), l.split('\t')[0].strip()) for l in out.split(NL) if '\t' in l)
+
+
+def read_remotes(paths):
+    for p in paths:
+        if repo_key(p) not in _REMOTE:
+            _REMOTE[repo_key(p)] = _ls(p)
+    for p in paths:
+        if 'refs/heads/main' not in _REMOTE[repo_key(p)]:
+            again = _ls(p)
+            REREAD.append('%s : first read empty ; re-read alone: %s' % (os.path.basename(p), 'refs read' if 'refs/heads/main' in again else '### EMPTY AGAIN'))
+            _REMOTE[repo_key(p)] = again
+
+
+def remote_refs(p):
+    return _REMOTE.get(repo_key(p), {})
+
+
+def remote_tag(p, t):
+    r = remote_refs(p)
+    if 'refs/tags/%s^{}' % t in r:
+        return r['refs/tags/%s^{}' % t], 'annotated, peeled'
+    if 'refs/tags/%s' % t in r:
+        return r['refs/tags/%s' % t], 'lightweight, its own line'
+    return '', '### NOT AT THE REMOTE'
+
+
+ACT = 'b715'
+CAPTURES = (('relay', 'relay', ROOT), ('PLACE-papers', 'pp', 'D:/MY-DOwnloads/PLACE-papers'))
+
+
+# ### (R325)(2)(k), a carry-time repair: the head-line builder's capture-name failures go to the events section (events_section) and never into
+# ### the head line; filled at each head_line call and read by the section beneath the defects.
+HEAD_EVENTS = []
+
+
+def _captures(dd, act):
+    """every push capture of the act but the closing's own (data/<act>_*push_out.txt), each read by its own lines: (name, its UTC stamp at :1,
+    the repository at :2, the branch, the tip, the main read back at the remote)."""
+    out = []
+    for n in sorted(os.listdir(dd)):
+        if not (n.startswith(act + '_') and n.endswith('push_out.txt')) or n == '%s_closing_push_out.txt' % act:
+            continue
+        t = io.open(os.path.join(dd, n), encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').read().replace(chr(13), '')
+        s = re.search(r'^push_gated: capture on -> \S+ \((\d{4}-\d\d-\d\dT\d\d:\d\d:\d\dZ)\)', t, re.M)
+        b = re.search(r'^push_gated: repo (\S+) ; branch (\S+) ; tip (\w+)', t, re.M)
+        m = re.search(r'^push_gated: main read back at the remote: (\w+)', t, re.M)
+        out.append((n, s.group(1) if s else None, b.group(1) if b else None, b.group(2) if b else None, b.group(3) if b else None,
+                    m.group(1) if m else None))
+    return out
+
+
+def head_line(dd=None, act=ACT, events=None):
+    """(R325)(2)(k), repaired: each repository's record commit is the main its LAST push capture of the act read back at the remote (the
+    closing's own capture excluded, the captures ordered by their own UTC stamps), printed in the head line; where b673's name for the record's
+    capture (data/<act>_<key>_push_out.txt) is not that capture, or no capture reads main back, the failure goes to the events section and the
+    head line carries the commit or `none read`, never the failure's text (b714's head line carried "IS NOT THE RECORD`S CAPTURE" for both)."""
+    dd = dd or D
+    ev = HEAD_EVENTS if events is None else events
+
+    def _t(n):
+        p = os.path.join(dd, n)
+        return io.open(p, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').read().replace(chr(13), '') if os.path.exists(p) else ''
+
+    # ### (R325)(4)(v), standing clauses v4 row (66): THE HEAD LINE READS EACH REPOSITORY'S MAIN AT THE REMOTE by ls-remote (one read per
+    # ### repository, an empty read re-read alone), not from the act's push captures -- b714 wrote no capture but the closing's, and its head line
+    # ### read "none read" for both (events :48). The captures are still read, and a remote main no capture of the act read back goes to the
+    # ### events section (a repair at b715, banked by diff).
+    caps = _captures(dd, act)
+    read_remotes([repo for _n, _k, repo in CAPTURES])
+    parts = []
+    for name, key, repo in CAPTURES:
+        w = 8 if key == 'relay' else 7
+        main = remote_refs(repo).get('refs/heads/main', '')
+        if not main:
+            ev.append('%s: ls-remote read no main at the remote, read twice; the head line carries none read' % name)
+            parts.append('%s none read' % name)
+            continue
+        mine = [c for c in caps if c[2] and c[5] and repo_key(c[2]) == repo_key(repo)]
+        if not any(c[5] == main for c in mine):
+            ev.append('%s: the remote`s main %s is read back by no push capture of the act (%d captures of the repository)' % (name, main[:w], len(mine)))
+        parts.append('%s %s' % (name, main[:w]))
+    sm = re.search(r'ARMS RUN : (\d+)\. ### LIVE PASSING : (\d+)\.', _t('%s_checks_postpush.txt' % act))
+    suite = ('suite %s of %s' % (sm.group(2), sm.group(1))) if sm else 'suite ### NOT READ'
+    try:
+        root = json.loads(_t('%s_act_root.json' % act))['root'][:16] + '…'
+    except Exception:
+        root = '### NOT READ'
+    # ### (R325)(3)(iii), standing clauses v3: the prompt count is the answers bank's rows with nothing added (b700's "+ 1" for the root's
+    # ### prompt counted it twice where the bank already holds it, b714's events :79), and the scores are read from the scores-close bank
+    k = len(re.findall(r'^### PROMPT ', _t('%s_author_answers.txt' % act), re.M))
+    d = len([l for l in _t('%s_defects.txt' % act).split(NL) if DEFECT_ROW.match(l)])
+    try:
+        sc = json.loads(_t('%s_scores_close.json' % act))
+    except Exception:
+        sc = {}
+    keys = [x for ks in SCORE_KEYS for x in ks]
+    held = sum(1 for x in keys if (sc.get(x) or [''])[0] == 'HELD')
+    scores = ('scores %d of %d HELD' % (held, len(keys))) if all(x in sc for x in keys) else 'scores ### NOT READ'
+    return '%s closed: %s; %s; root %s; %d prompt%s answered; %s; %d defect%s' % (act, ', '.join(parts), suite, root, k, '' if k == 1 else 's',
+                                                                                 scores, d, '' if d == 1 else 's')
+
+
+def events_section(act=ACT, dd=None):
+    """(R325)(2)(i), standing clauses v2 row (52): a finding made after the seal is entered here and not lettered in the defects bank (the next
+    act's corrigendum letters it) -- the rows of data/<act>_events.txt as the seat entered them (by the Write or the Edit tool; every line of
+    the bank from its third, indented), and the head-line builder's capture-name failures (HEAD_EVENTS)."""
+    p = os.path.join(dd or D, '%s_events.txt' % act)
+    t = io.open(p, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').read().replace(chr(13), '') if os.path.exists(p) else ''
+    rows = [l for l in t.split(NL)[2:] if l.startswith('    ')]
+    return ['### THE EVENTS AFTER THE SEAL (standing clauses v2 row (52); relay data/%s_events.txt, unlettered, for the next act`s corrigendum):' % act] + (
+        rows or ['    NONE ENTERED IN THE EVENTS BANK']) + ['    THE HEAD LINE: ' + e for e in HEAD_EVENTS] + [
+        '    ### EVENTS %d ; THE HEAD LINE`S %d' % (len([l for l in rows if not l.startswith('     ')]), len(HEAD_EVENTS))]
+
+
+def citation_line():
+    cc, cj, dl = rd('b715_citation_check.txt'), jl('b715_citation_check.json'), rd('b715_defects.txt')
+    head = verdict(cc, r'(?<!ANSWER )CITES CHECKED \d+ ; OK \d+ ; MISSES \d+')
+    misses = cj.get('misses') or []
+    needles = re.findall(r"^### \(R278\)\(4\) THE MID-ACT CHECK \([^)]*\): the (?:navigator`s )?answer carrying '([^']*)'", cc, re.M)
+    figs = re.findall(r'ANSWER CITES CHECKED \d+ ; OK \d+ ; MISSES \d+', cc)
+    nav = re.findall(r"^    \(([a-z]'?)\) THE NAVIGATOR'S", dl, re.M)
+    return ('%s ; the misses as the bank lists them: %d (%s) ; the answers checked ((R278)(4)), by their needles: %s, the prints %s ; entered as '
+            'the defects bank letters them: the navigator`s %s' % (
+                head, len(misses), '; '.join(str(m)[:60] for m in misses) or 'NONE', '; '.join(needles) or 'NONE', '; '.join(figs) or 'NONE',
+                ', '.join('(%s)' % x for x in nav) or 'NONE'))
+
+
+def state_file_sentence(path='tools/mirror_prevbuild.json', repo=ROOT):
+    """(R325)(3)(v): the closing's sentence about a state file read from its blob against the last commit before it says "committed": the
+    working file's blob (git hash-object) against HEAD's blob of the path and the last commit that touched it."""
+    work = g(repo, 'hash-object', '--', path).strip()
+    head = g(repo, 'rev-parse', '--verify', '--quiet', 'HEAD:' + path).strip()
+    last = g(repo, 'log', '-1', '--format=%h %s', '--', path).strip()
+    if work and head and work == head:
+        return '%s unchanged since its last commit %s (blob %s, equal at HEAD and in the working tree), not committed with this closing' % (
+            path, last[:8] or '### NONE', work[:8])
+    return '%s written at the build (blob %s, HEAD`s %s), committed with this closing' % (path, work[:8] or '### NONE', head[:8] or '### NONE')
+
+
+def mirror_line():
+    j = jl('b715_mirror.json')
+    if not j:
+        return '### TO BE BUILT AFTER THE ROOT AND THE LAST PLACE-papers PUSH (the b670 form, mirror-refresh-%s-b715.zip; over b714`s roster of 70 entries, the builder`s state file tools/mirror_prevbuild.json named in (W))' % WL.DATE
+    return '%s ; %d bytes ; sha256 %s ; MANIFEST md5 %s ; entries %d ; %s ; %s ; %s ; the roster b714`s, 70 entries; %s' % (
+        j.get('zip'), j.get('bytes', 0), (j.get('zip_sha256') or '')[:16], j.get('manifest_md5'), j.get('entries', 0), j.get('root_line') or '### NO ROOT LINE',
+        'CLEAN ON ALL THREE CLAUSES' if j.get('clean') else '### NOT CLEAN', j.get('span') or '### NO SPAN LINE', state_file_sentence())
+
+
+def scores_at_close():
+    S, C = jl('b715_scores.json'), jl('b715_scores_close.json')
+    out = {}
+    for ks in SCORE_KEYS:
+        for k in ks:
+            s = S.get(k) or ['?', '']
+            c = C.get(k)
+            out[k] = (c, 'close') if (s[0] == 'PENDING' and c) else (s, 'record')
+    return out
+
+
+# ================================================================================ THE STANDING REPORT ((R325)(3): its lines in footing order, from the unsealed report tool's bank)
+def report_lines():
+    rj = jl('b715_report.json')
+    ls = rj.get('lines') or []
+    if len(ls) >= 7:
+        return ['### THE STANDING REPORT ((R325)(3), its lines in footing order KERNEL, CERTIFIED, REGISTERED, HELD; relay data/b715_report.txt, by the unsealed tools/b715_report.py before the root; its scores line printed after the root by `tools/b715_report.py scores`, written nowhere):'] + [
+            '    (%d) %s' % (i + 1, l[:900]) for i, l in enumerate(ls)]
+    return ['### THE STANDING REPORT ((R325)(3)): ### data/b715_report.txt NOT YET WRITTEN (tools/b715_report.py bank runs before the root)']
+

+def _guard(name):
+    """(R325)(2)(ii), standing clauses v2 row (53), the membrane: a write to relay data/ refused once the act's root is appended, unless the
+    act's ROOT_EXCLUDE takes the bank out (the closing's banks, its censuses and pins are excluded kinds); the dry run writes to the scratchpad."""

+          '    pre-push : %s' % verdict(pre, r'ARMS RUN : \d+\. ### LIVE PASSING : \d+\. ### LIVE FAILING : \d+[^*]*'),
+          '    pre-push : %s' % verdict(pre, r'VERDICT : [A-Z ]+'),
+          '    post-push : %s' % verdict(post, r'ARMS RUN : \d+\. ### LIVE PASSING : \d+\. ### LIVE FAILING : \d+[^*]*'),
+          '    post-push : %s' % verdict(post, r'VERDICT : [A-Z ]+')]
+    L += heads
+    L += ['    censuses : %s / %s' % (verdict(rd('b715_census_closing.txt') if not DRY else io.open(os.path.join(OUTD, 'b715_census_closing.txt'), encoding='utf-8').read(),
+                                               r'TOTAL MISSING : \d+'),
+                                       verdict(rd('b715_faces_census_closing.txt') if not DRY else io.open(os.path.join(OUTD, 'b715_faces_census_closing.txt'), encoding='utf-8').read(),
+                                               r'TOTAL MISSING : \d+')),
+          '    pins : %s' % verdict(rd('b715_pins_closing.txt') if not DRY else io.open(os.path.join(OUTD, 'b715_pins_closing.txt'), encoding='utf-8').read(),
+                                    r'REPOS HARD-FAILING : \d+')]
+    L += KERNEL_LINES + ['']
+    L += ['### CARRIED FORWARD.'] + carried() + ['=' * 104]
+    b = (NL.join(L) + NL).encode('utf-8')
+    _guard('b715_closing.txt')
+    # ### (R325)(4)(iv) and (vi), standing clauses v4 rows (65) and (68), a repair at b715: the dry run is step [3] of the close's order and
+    # ### writes its bank to relay data/ (b714 wrote it to the scratchpad, so its order left no evidence of the step), refused once the lock
+    # ### is written; the closing is step [25], refused while a lower step's evidence is absent
+    import act_close as AC
+    r = AC.lock_refuses(ACT, 'b715_closing_dry.txt') if DRY else AC.close_refuses(ACT, 25)
+    if r:
+        print(r, flush=True)
+        sys.exit(5)
+    p = os.path.join(D, 'b715_closing_dry.txt') if DRY else os.path.join(OUTD, 'b715_closing.txt')
+    with open(p + '.tmp', 'wb') as fh:
+        fh.write(b)
+    os.replace(p + '.tmp', p)
+    if DRY:
+        print('  ### THE CLOSING DRY-RUN, its readings (written to relay data/, %s):' % p)
+        for l in L:
+            if l.startswith(('b715 closed', '### THE', '    the', '    (g)')):
+                print('    ' + l[:400])
+        print('  ### ### **THE CLOSING DRY-RUN : READ**')
+    else:
+        print(NL.join(L[-60:]))
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    if len(sys.argv) > 2 and sys.argv[1] == 'defects-row':
+        print(defects_row(sys.argv[2]))
+    else:
+        main()

+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""b715_corrigendum.py -- THE CORRIGENDUM ENTRY OF (R325)(2): b714's events after its seal (relay data/b714_events.txt, twelve starting
+lines) and the two items the navigator adds, answered by one generated FINDINGS entry in b678's corrigendum form, in the banks' words, every
+item carrying a column NEW or REPEAT. Nine are lettered as defects, each with its owner, (r)-(z) after b714's seventeen letters (a)-(q) (relay
+data/b714_defects.txt), the letters from tools/act_letters.py (the one lettering the suite imports too); five are entered as records,
+unlettered. Each lettered item names the repair of (R325)(3) or (4) that takes it up, by its line in relay data/b715_ferry.txt, or reads
+"carried" with its reason; the repairs' diffs and two-instance controls are banked at Components 2 and 3 in data/b715_tool_repairs.txt.
+b714's events bank, its defects bank, its sealed face, its trail record (PLACE-papers OPEN_TRAILS :15962) and every root-named bank unedited.
+
+### THE NEW OR REPEAT COLUMN: an item is REPEAT where the corrigenda bank (relay data/b675_corrigenda.txt, rows (1)-(34), the record of every
+### earlier act's lettered corrections since b675) carries an item of its class, and the column names that act, its letter and the row; the
+### row's text is read and its needle checked before any write. NEW where no row carries one. The evidence set is that bank alone.
+### The added items' sources: the ferry :9 (relay data/b715_ferry.txt), the author's word (5) (relay data/b714_author_words_preseal.txt :83),
+### b714's records (15)-(17) (relay data/b714_records.txt :91, :97, :107) and its event (5) (relay data/b714_events.txt :22), read at compose time.
+### Written whole by the Write tool at b715 in b714's form (tools/b714_corrigendum.py); b678's reader `rd` IMPORTED unchanged. Routes: `dry` ->
+### the entry printed through the screens, nothing written; `land` -> the entry appended to FINDINGS through b715_record's guarded landing,
+### data/b715_corrigendum.txt (.json). The corrigenda bank's row (35) is appended by the seat through the Edit tool from the line this tool
+### prints (ROW). It writes those two banks and the one FINDINGS entry.
+"""
+import json

+import re
+import sys
+import time

+NL = chr(10)
+if hasattr(sys.stdout, 'reconfigure'):

+TITLE = ('## Corrigendum to b714’s record after its seal (relay data/b714_events.txt): twelve events and two added items, nine lettered '
+         '(r)-(z) with their owners, five entered as records, each NEW or REPEAT, under (R325)(2)')
+PREV_LETTERS = list('abcdefghijklmnopq')

+# ### owner, the repair's (R325) clause or None for carried, the item's words, the column: None for NEW or (act, letter, corrigenda row, the
+# ### row's needle), the reason where carried)
+LETTERED = (

+     'the five are two omissions of the seat’s and three predicates of the copied suite, and (R325) names no repair for them; the '
+     'replay of (R325)(3)(iv) reads the same predicates over b714’s banks, and each that differs there is entered as a finding of the old tool'),
+    (22, 'STEP [18] RAISED, NOTHING WRITTEN; HALTED', 'THE RECORD TOOL’S', '4(iv)', 'the components route raising on a predecessor’s worklist key',
+     ('b713', 'q', 34, '(q) the next act, (r) the chain row count and (s) the root-check range carried unbumped into the sealed suite'), None),
+    (28, "THE SEAT'S: A ROOT ITEM EDITED AFTER THE ROOT", 'THE SEAT’S', None, 'a root-named bank edited after the root by the Edit tool and restored',
+     ('b698', 'd', 12, 'the Edit tool restored it'),
+     'the Edit tool’s write passes through no tool’s membrane, and (R325) names no repair for it; the bank is equal to its blob at the '
+     'root’s commit, and the class stands for the author’s ruling'),
+    (52, 'THE UNSEALED TOOLS AT THE CLOSE', 'THE CLOSING TOOL’S', '3(iii)', 'the sealed closing naming one unsealed tool by fixed text',
+     ('b711', 't', 32, 'for an unchanged blob, the closing tool`s'), None),
+    (66, 'THE POST-PUSH SUITE', 'THE HOOK’S', '4(iii)', 'a refused command appended to a root-named bank after the root',
+     ('b710', 'j', 31, '(j) a root-named bank written after the root, repaired by the membrane'), None),

+     ('b699', 'e', 16, "Lettered the navigator's (e)"), None),

+     ('b713', 'q', 34, '(q) the next act, (r) the chain row count and (s) the root-check range carried unbumped into the sealed suite'), None),

+# ### the records: (events line, the head's needle, the words, the column)
+RECORDS = (
+    (3, "THE ROOT'S PROMPT DISPLAYED CUT", 'the root’s prompt displayed cut at its second sentence', None),
+    (9, "THE REPORT TOOL'S TWO LINES RESTATED BEFORE ITS BANK", 'the unsealed report tool’s two lines restated before its bank',
+     ('b711', 'm', 32, '(m) the report tool`s two misreads')),

+    (63, "THE HARNESS'S AUTO-MODE NOTE AGAIN", 'the harness note not followed', ('b712', ':41', 33, 'Records, unlettered: :18, :41')),
+    (80, "THE CLOSE'S STEPS [20]-[24]", 'the close’s steps [20]-[24], the closing’s dry run refused after the lock', None),

+HALTS = ((91, '(15) THE SEAL REFUSED, NOTHING RECORDED'), (97, "(16) THE AUTHOR'S WORD AT THE SEAL, AND THE SEAL'S TOOL WITH NO ROUTE FOR IT"),
+         (107, '(17) THE WORD (6) CARRIED IN THE CHECKS TOOL; THE SEAL HELD ON ITS LAST SENTENCE'))
+WORD5_LINE = 83
+ROW = ('  (35) b714\'s record after its seal (relay data/b714_events.txt, twelve starting lines, and the two items (R325)(2) adds): lettered\n'
+       '      (r) the entry`s head clause composed against the author`s word, the record tool`s, carried; (s) five arms failing on real state\n'
+       '      beyond the face at the pre-push suite, the suite`s and the seat`s, carried; (t) the components route raising on a predecessor`s\n'
+       '      worklist key, the record tool`s, by (R325)(4)(iv); (u) a root-named bank edited after the root by the Edit tool and restored, the\n'
+       '      seat`s, carried; (v) the sealed closing naming one unsealed tool by fixed text, the closing tool`s, by (3)(iii); (w) a refused\n'
+       '      command appended to a root-named bank after the root, the hook`s, by (4)(iii); (x) the sealed closing carrying no line for the\n'
+       '      withheld arm, the closing tool`s, and (y) word (5)`s clause "no tool is edited for this", the navigator`s, both by (4)(ii); (z) the\n'
+       '      act`s four halts at the seal and at step [18], one class, a close computed in copied tools, the copied tools`, by (3). Records,\n'
+       '      unlettered: :3, :9, :33, :63, :80. NEW OR REPEAT: REPEAT %s ; THE REPEAT FRACTION %s.\n'
+       '      THE CORRECTION: the standing close toolset of (R325)(3) and the class repairs of (R325)(4), their diffs and controls\n'
+       '      data/b715_tool_repairs.txt. The generated corrigendum entry stands at FINDINGS.md :%s.')

+def _flat(t):
+    return re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', t)
+
+
+def _corrigenda_rows():
+    """the corrigenda bank's rows by number, each row's lines joined and its whitespace collapsed."""
+    rows, cur = {}, None
+    for l in C78.rd(os.path.join(D, 'b675_corrigenda.txt')).replace(chr(13), '').split(NL):
+        m = re.match(r'^  \((\d+)\) ', l)
+        if m:
+            cur = int(m.group(1))
+            rows[cur] = l
+        elif cur is not None and l.startswith('      '):
+            rows[cur] += ' ' + l.strip()
+        elif not l.strip() or l.startswith('###'):
+            cur = None
+    return dict((k, _flat(v)) for k, v in rows.items())
+
+
+def compose():
+    ev = C78.rd(os.path.join(D, 'b714_events.txt')).replace(chr(13), '').split(NL)
+    starts = [i for i, l in enumerate(ev, 1) if re.match(r'^\(\d+\) ', l)]
+    want_starts = sorted([x[0] for x in LETTERED if x[0]] + [x[0] for x in RECORDS])
+    heads = dict((n, re.sub(r'^\(\d+\) ', '', ev[n - 1])) for n in starts)
+    miss_heads = [x[0] for x in LETTERED if x[0] and not heads.get(x[0], '').startswith(x[1])] + \
+                 [x[0] for x in RECORDS if not heads.get(x[0], '').startswith(x[1])]
+    dt = C78.rd(os.path.join(D, 'b714_defects.txt'))
+    prev = [re.match(r"\(([a-z]'?)\)", x).group(1) for x in REC.letters_of(dt)]
+    letters = AL.after(prev, len(LETTERED)) if prev == PREV_LETTERS else []
+    cr = _corrigenda_rows()
+    cols = [x[5] for x in LETTERED] + [x[3] for x in RECORDS]
+    bad_cols = [(c[0], c[1], c[2]) for c in cols if c and _flat(c[3]) not in cr.get(c[2], '')]
+    f15 = C78.rd(os.path.join(D, 'b715_ferry.txt')).split(NL)
+    l9 = f15[8] if len(f15) > 8 else ''
+    added = [l9.count(x[1]) for x in LETTERED if x[0] is None]
+    w5 = C78.rd(os.path.join(D, 'b714_author_words_preseal.txt')).split(NL)
+    w5_ok = len(w5) >= WORD5_LINE and 'no tool is edited for this' in w5[WORD5_LINE - 1]
+    rc = C78.rd(os.path.join(D, 'b714_records.txt')).split(NL)
+    halts_ok = [n for n, h in HALTS if not (len(rc) >= n and rc[n - 1].startswith(h))]
+    ferry_ok = [k for k, n in FERRY_LINES.items() if not (len(f15) >= n and f15[n - 1].startswith(FERRY_NEEDLES[k]))]
+    s = dict(starts=starts, heads=heads, prev=prev, letters=letters, added=added, w5=w5_ok,
+             corrigenda_last=max(cr) if cr else 0)
+    bad = [k for k, v in (('the twelve starting lines', starts == want_starts), ('each item`s head', not miss_heads),
+                          ('b714`s seventeen letters', prev == PREV_LETTERS), ('the nine letters after them', letters == list('rstuvwxyz')),
+                          ('each REPEAT`s row and needle', not bad_cols), ('the ferry :9 carrying each added item once', added == [1, 1]),
+                          ('word (5)`s clause at its line', w5_ok), ('the three halts` records at their lines', not halts_ok),
+                          ('each repair`s ferry line', not ferry_ok),
+                          ('the corrigenda bank`s last row (34)', max(cr) == 34 if cr else False)) if not v]
+    if bad:
+        sys.exit('### THE BANKS NOT AS READ: %s -- NOTHING COMPOSED (heads missed %s ; columns %s ; starts %s ; added %s ; halts %s ; ferry %s)' % (
+            bad, miss_heads, bad_cols, starts, added, halts_ok, ferry_ok))
+    return s
+
+
+def _column(c):
+    if not c:
+        return 'NEW (no row of relay data/b675_corrigenda.txt carries its class)'
+    act, letter, row, _n = c
+    what = ('%s’s record %s' % (act, letter)) if letter.startswith(':') else ('%s (%s)' % (act, AL.canon(letter)))
+    return 'REPEAT of %s, relay data/b675_corrigenda.txt row (%d)' % (what, row)
+
+
+def fraction(s=None):
+    """the repeat fraction: (REPEAT among the lettered, lettered; REPEAT among all items, all items)."""
+    rl = sum(1 for x in LETTERED if x[5])
+    ra = rl + sum(1 for x in RECORDS if x[3])
+    return rl, len(LETTERED), ra, len(LETTERED) + len(RECORDS)
+
+
+def fraction_words():
+    rl, nl, ra, na = fraction()
+    return '%d of %d (%.2f) ; among the lettered %d of %d (%.2f)' % (ra, na, ra / float(na), rl, nl, rl / float(nl))
+
+
+def entry_text(s):
+    return NL + NL.join(_entry_lines(s)) + NL
+
+
+def _repair_words(r, why):
+    if not r:
+        return 'CARRIED: %s.' % why
+    return ('TAKEN UP BY (R325)(%s) (relay data/b715_ferry.txt :%d); its diff and its two-instance control in relay '
+            'data/b715_tool_repairs.txt.' % (r.replace('(', ')(', 1), FERRY_LINES[r]))
+
+
+WHY = {
+    6: ('The sealed record tool’s _finding_text (tools/b714_record.py :2307-:2308) composed “held in its bank and carried, the author’s word '

+        'clause landed as composed at FINDINGS :10512.'),

+         'G-ANSWERS-BEFORE-WRITES on no mid-act answer check before the root, the seat’s; G-ENTRY-TITLE-FORM on a needle written before the '

+         'seat’s (data/b714_events.txt :10-:21).'),
+    22: ('`tools/b714_record.py components` passed its order gate and raised AttributeError on the worklist key C1_PP_FACES (:2643): the route '
+         '(:2630-:2656, sealed) was b713’s composer carried unrewritten; data/b714_components.txt was written by a stand-in tool on the author’s '

+         'data/b714_hook_refusals.txt, to which the hook appended a refused command after the root; the hook writes its refusals to a bank '
+         'the root names.'),
+    75: ('The combined pre-run read 100 passing, of which G-RULE-LONG-PHASE-SPLIT was since WITHHELD, NOT EXERCISABLE; the sealed closing '
+         'carries no line for a withheld arm (no `withheld` in tools/b714_closing.py), so the fact stood in the events bank alone.'),
+    'no tool is edited for this': ('Word (5) took route 2, the arm withheld at the seal, and closed “no tool is edited for this” '
+                                   '(relay data/b714_author_words_preseal.txt :83); the seal’s tool had no withheld path, so the route '
+                                   'could not be carried out unedited, and word (6) edited the checks tool for it.'),
+    'a close computed in copied tools': ('The seal refused and the act halted at step [8] (relay data/b714_records.txt (15) :91), halted '
+                                         'there again for want of a route (16) :97 and held on its last sentence (17) :107; step [18] raised and '
+                                         'halted (data/b714_events.txt (5) :22). Each halt stood in a per-act tool copied from its predecessor '
+                                         'and carrying that act’s path or objects.'),
+}

+def _entry_lines(s):
+    L = [TITLE, '',
+         ('b714’s events bank (relay data/b714_events.txt) carries twelve starting lines (:%s), entered after its seal and not lettered, '
+          'for this act’s corrigendum under standing clauses v2 row (52); (R325)(2) adds two items, the navigator’s. Seven events and the two '
+          'added items are lettered as defects, each with its owner; b714’s defects bank carries (a)-(q), so these are (r)-(z), lettered by '
+          'relay tools/act_letters.py. Five are entered as records. Each item carries NEW or REPEAT, a REPEAT naming the earlier act and letter '
+          'of its class and the row of relay data/b675_corrigenda.txt that records it; the evidence set is that bank’s rows (1)-(%d). Each '
+          'lettered item names the repair of (R325)(3) or (4) that takes it up, or reads CARRIED with its reason. b714’s events bank, its '
+          'defects bank, its face, its trail record (PLACE-papers OPEN_TRAILS :%d) and every root-named bank are not edited.' % (
+              ', :'.join(str(n) for n in s['starts']), s['corrigenda_last'], K.B711_TRAIL)), '']
+    for (n, h, own, r, words, col, why), x in zip(LETTERED, s['letters']):
+        place = 'relay data/b714_events.txt :%d' % n if n else 'the item (R325)(2) adds'
+        L += ['**(%s) %s** (%s; %s; %s). %s %s' % (x, words[0].upper() + words[1:], place, own, _column(col), WHY[n if n else h],
+                                                   _repair_words(r, why)), '']
+    L += ['**Records, unlettered:** ' + '; '.join(':%d %s (%s)' % (n, w, _column(c)) for n, _h, w, c in RECORDS) +
+          ' (relay data/b714_events.txt).', '',
+          '**The repeat fraction:** REPEAT %s.' % fraction_words(), '',
+          'The sealed face, the events and defects banks, the trail record and every root-named bank are not edited -- this entry is their '
+          'correction. Nothing here proves RH or GRH.', '',
+          ('*Filed at b715 on the author’s ruling `(R325)`(2): generated by relay `tools/b715_corrigendum.py`, written whole at b715 in the '
+           'corrigendum form of relay `tools/b678_corrigendum.py` (its reader imported unchanged), its letters from relay `tools/act_letters.py`, '
+           'banked as relay `data/b715_corrigendum.txt`, the corrigenda bank `data/b675_corrigenda.txt` appended at row (35); it answers b714’s '
+           'events after its seal.*')]
+    return L
+
+
+def bank_lines(s, landed_line=None):
+    L = ['b715 -- THE CORRIGENDUM ENTRY FOR b714`S EVENTS AFTER ITS SEAL, (R325)(2) (%s)' % time.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ', time.gmtime()), '',
+         '### the figures read before the write: the starting lines :%s ; b714`s letters %s ; the letters after them %s ;' % (
+             ', :'.join(str(n) for n in s['starts']), ''.join(s['prev']), ''.join(s['letters'])),
+         '### the ferry :9 carrying each added item %s time(s) ; word (5)`s clause at :%d %s ; the halts` records %s and event (5) :22 ; the '
+         'corrigenda bank`s last row (%d).' % (s['added'], WORD5_LINE, s['w5'], [n for n, _h in HALTS], s['corrigenda_last']), '']
+    L += ['  ' + l for l in _entry_lines(s)]
+    L += ['', '### ### **ITEMS %d: LETTERED %d (r)-(z), EACH WITH ITS OWNER AND ITS REPAIR OR CARRIED ; RECORDS %d ; REPEAT %s ; '
+              'THE FACE, THE EVENTS AND DEFECTS BANKS, THE TRAIL AND THE ROOT-NAMED BANKS UNEDITED ; THE ENTRY AT FINDINGS :%s.**' % (
+                  len(LETTERED) + len(RECORDS), len(LETTERED), len(RECORDS), fraction_words(), landed_line if landed_line else 'NOT YET WRITTEN')]
+    return L
+
+
+def _membrane(p):
+    """standing clauses v2 row (53): the membrane before a write under relay data/ (tools/act_membrane.py guard_path)."""
+    import act_membrane as MEM
+    MEM.guard_path('b715', p, REC.R.ROOT_EXCLUDE)
+
+
+def _put(name, data):
+    p = os.path.join(D, name)
+    _membrane(p)
+    with open(p + '.tmp', 'wb') as fh:
+        fh.write(data.encode('utf-8'))
+    os.replace(p + '.tmp', p)
+
+
+def _row_text(ln):
+    rl, nl, ra, na = fraction()
+    return ROW % ('%d of %d' % (ra, na), '%.2f, among the lettered %d of %d' % (ra / float(na), rl, nl), ln)
+
+
+def main(route):
+    s = compose()
+    text = entry_text(s)
+    if route == 'dry':
+        REC.R.screens([('FINDINGS.md', TITLE, conv(text))], 'corrigendum', True)
+        print(conv(text))
+        print(NL.join(bank_lines(s)))
+        print(_row_text('NOT YET WRITTEN'))
+        return
+    REC.land([('FINDINGS.md', TITLE, text)], 'b715_corrigendum_landing.json', 'corrigendum', entry=None, dry=False)
+    ln = (REC.jl('b715_corrigendum_landing.json').get('lines') or [{}])[0].get('line')
+    L = bank_lines(s, ln)
+    _put('b715_corrigendum.txt', NL.join(L) + NL)
+    j = dict(at=time.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ', time.gmtime()), entry_line=ln, title=TITLE,
+             lettered=[dict(letter=x, line=n, owner=o, repair=r, ferry_line=FERRY_LINES.get(r) if r else None,
+                            column=('REPEAT' if c else 'NEW'), repeat_of=(list(c[:3]) if c else None), carried=(why if not r else None))
+                       for (n, _h, o, r, _w, c, why), x in zip(LETTERED, s['letters'])],
+             records=[dict(line=n, column=('REPEAT' if c else 'NEW'), repeat_of=(list(c[:3]) if c else None)) for n, _h, _w, c in RECORDS],
+             starts=s['starts'], row=35, fraction=list(fraction()))
+    _put('b715_corrigendum.json', json.dumps(j, indent=1, ensure_ascii=False) + NL)
+    print(L[-1])
+    print(_row_text(ln))
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    main(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 'dry')

+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""b715_import_closure.py -- THE STANDING TOOLSET'S SIZE, MEASURED (the author's word (1)(d), relay data/b715_author_words_preseal.txt): the
+transitive set of tools/*.py modules b714's fourteen close tools import, each with its line count and its act-literal count (the pattern
+\\bb\\d{3}\\b over the whole source, comments and docstrings counted), banked as data/b715_import_closure.txt. The scratchpad probe of
+data/b715_records.txt (4) carried into relay's tools/ beside the toolset, its walk and its counts unchanged, run once more here; its foot is
+read against the record's figures (197 modules, 166 act-named, 31 shared, 134978 lines, 12883 literals) and printed EQUAL or DIFFERS.
+Written whole by the Write tool at b715. Route: `bank` (the one write, through the membrane) or `dry` (prints, writes nothing).
+"""
+import os
+import re
+import sys
+import time
+
+ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
+D, T = os.path.join(ROOT, 'data'), os.path.join(ROOT, 'tools')
+sys.path.insert(0, T)
+NL = chr(10)
+if hasattr(sys.stdout, 'reconfigure'):
+    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+
+SEEDS = ['b714_worklist', 'b714_record', 'b714_checks', 'b714_closing', 'b714_citation', 'b714_tests', 'b714_standing',
+         'b714_corrigendum', 'b714_regspec', 'b714_reg_gate', 'b714_report', 'b714_hook', 'b714_repairs', 'b714_carry']
+IMP = re.compile(r'^\s*(?:import|from)\s+([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)', re.M)
+LIT = re.compile(r'\bb\d{3}\b')
+RECORD = dict(modules=197, act=166, shared=31, lines=134978, literals=12883)   # ### data/b715_records.txt (4), the scratchpad run
+
+
+def src(m):
+    p = os.path.join(T, m + '.py')
+    return open(p, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').read() if os.path.isfile(p) else None
+
+
+def walk():
+    seen, todo = {}, list(SEEDS)
+    while todo:
+        m = todo.pop()
+        if m in seen:
+            continue
+        s = src(m)
+        if s is None:
+            continue
+        seen[m] = s
+        for n in IMP.findall(s):
+            if n not in seen and os.path.isfile(os.path.join(T, n + '.py')):
+                todo.append(n)
+    return seen
+
+
+def lines():
+    seen = walk()
+    act = sorted(m for m in seen if re.match(r'^b\d{3}_', m))
+    shared = sorted(m for m in seen if not re.match(r'^b\d{3}_', m))
+    got = dict(modules=len(seen), act=len(act), shared=len(shared), lines=sum(seen[m].count('\n') for m in seen),
+               literals=sum(len(LIT.findall(seen[m])) for m in seen))
+    L = ['b715 -- THE STANDING TOOLSET`S SIZE: THE MODULES b714`S CLOSE IMPORTS, TRANSITIVELY, WITH LINES AND ACT LITERALS (%s)' %
+         time.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ', time.gmtime()), '',
+         '### rows are modules of relay tools/, act-named first then shared, each: the module | lines (newlines counted) | act literals (the',
+         '### pattern \\bb\\d{3}\\b over the whole source). The walk from b714`s fourteen tools: %s.' % ' '.join(SEEDS),
+         '### Written by tools/b715_import_closure.py (the author`s word (1)(d)); the scratchpad probe`s walk and counts unchanged.', '']
+    L += ['  %-34s | lines %6d | act literals %5d' % (m, seen[m].count('\n'), len(LIT.findall(seen[m]))) for m in act + shared]
+    eq = ['%s %s %s' % (k, got[k], 'EQUAL' if got[k] == RECORD[k] else 'DIFFERS (the record %s)' % RECORD[k]) for k in RECORD]
+    L += ['', '### against data/b715_records.txt (4), the scratchpad run: ' + ' ; '.join(eq),
+          '', '### ### **MODULES %d (ACT-NAMED %d, SHARED %d) ; LINES %d ; ACT LITERALS %d ; AGAINST THE RECORD %s.**' % (
+              got['modules'], got['act'], got['shared'], got['lines'], got['literals'],
+              'EQUAL' if all(got[k] == RECORD[k] for k in RECORD) else 'DIFFERS')]
+    return L
+
+
+def main(route):
+    L = lines()
+    if route == 'bank':
+        import act_membrane as MEM
+        import b715_record as REC
+        p = os.path.join(D, 'b715_import_closure.txt')
+        MEM.guard_path('b715', p, REC.R.ROOT_EXCLUDE)
+        with open(p + '.tmp', 'wb') as fh:
+            fh.write((NL.join(L) + NL).encode('utf-8'))
+        os.replace(p + '.tmp', p)
+    print(NL.join(L[-3:]))
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    main(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 'dry')
