+_NOHB = (120, 99, 100, 103, 104, 105, 107, 108, 110, 111, 112, 113, 114, 116, 117)   # ### (P3)'s NO HOMEBREW ROW lines
+_KW = re.compile(r'^\s*(?:@\[[^\]]*\]\s*)?(?:private\s+|protected\s+|noncomputable\s+)*(theorem|lemma|def|abbrev|structure|class|instance|axiom|opaque)\b')
+
+
+def _chain_read(repo, pin, path, line, kind):
+    """(sha, statement, kind, span end, sorry lines) of the declaration at `path`:`line` at `pin`, from git show; None when unread."""
+    sha = (gitc(repo, 'rev-parse', '--short=7', pin + '^{commit}') or '').strip()
+    src = gitc(repo, 'show', '%s:%s' % (pin, path))
+    if not src:
+        return None
+    ls = src.replace('\r', '').split(NL)
+    i = line - 1
+    if kind == 'field':
+        ind = len(ls[i]) - len(ls[i].lstrip())
+        j = i + 1
+        while j < len(ls) and ls[j].strip() and len(ls[j]) - len(ls[j].lstrip()) > ind:
+            j += 1
+        return sha, NL.join(ls[i:j]), 'field', j, []
+    m = _KW.match(ls[i])
+    k = m.group(1) if m else '?'
+    j = i
+    while j < len(ls) and not re.search(r':=|\bwhere\b', ls[j]):
+        j += 1
+    st = re.split(r':=|\bwhere\b', NL.join(ls[i:j + 1]), 1)[0].rstrip()
+    if k in ('def', 'abbrev'):
+        e = j + 1
+        while e < len(ls) and ls[e].strip() and not _KW.match(ls[e]) and not ls[e].lstrip().startswith('/-'):
+            e += 1
+        st = NL.join(ls[i:e]).rstrip()
+    e = line
+    while e < len(ls) and not _KW.match(ls[e]) and not re.match(r'^\s*(end|namespace|section)\b', ls[e]):
+        e += 1
+    sl = [line + n for n, t in enumerate(ls[line - 1:e]) if re.search(r'(?<![\w`])sorry\b', t.split('--')[0])]
+    return sha, st, k, e, sl
+
+
+def gitc(repo, *a):
+    r = subprocess.run(['git', '-C', repo] + list(a), capture_output=True, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+    return r.stdout if r.returncode == 0 else None
+
+
+def _chain_axioms(ax, sha):
+    """(text, source) of a row's axioms: a probe bank counts only when its head names the pin's commit and reads no DIFF."""
+    if ax[0] == 'none':
+        return 'axioms not read at this pin', ax[1]
+    t = (rdn(ax[1]) or '').split(NL)
+    if ax[0] == 'probe':
+        head = t[:4]
+        if not head or sha not in head[0] or any('DIFF' in h for h in head[1:3]):
+            return 'axioms not read at this pin', 'relay data/%s :1-:3 -- %s' % (ax[1], 'the head names another commit' if not head or sha not in head[0]
+                                                                                   else 'the pin`s file printed over a checkout whose file or imports DIFF')
+        return ' '.join(' '.join(t[ax[2] - 1:ax[3]]).split()), 'relay data/%s :%d%s, its head naming %s with no DIFF' % (
+            ax[1], ax[2], '-:%d' % ax[3] if ax[3] != ax[2] else '', sha)
+    return ' '.join(t[ax[2] - 1].split()), 'relay data/%s :%d' % (ax[1], ax[2])
+
+
+def chainrows(*a):
+    """(R325)(7)'s evidence for the census v0.8.2's rows: each of the 24 declarations of (P3) read at its pin, its statement, its grade under
+    the shared E0 rule (tools/e0_rule.py grade), the sorry lines of its span and its axioms where a banked print at the pin reaches them.
+    Banked as data/b715_chain_rows.txt; the census rows are written from it by the Edit tool."""
+    import e0_rule as E0
+    L = ['b715 -- COMPONENT 5, (R325)(7): THE CHAIN`S 24 DECLARATIONS READ AT THEIR PINS, GRADED AND THEIR AXIOMS READ (%s)' % utc(), '',
+         '### rows are declarations, in the order (R325)(7) takes them (the pilot`s NO HOMEBREW ROW names first, rh_strip_imp_rh_holds among them, then',
+         '### the rest): the pilot`s line (relay data/b714_pilot.txt), the name, the repository, pin and place, the statement at the pin (git show',
+         '### <pin>:<path>, from its line to its `:=`; a def whole), its kind, the E0 rule`s grade and why (tools/e0_rule.py grade, from the statement',
+         '### alone), the span`s sorry lines and the axioms. Written by tools/b715_record.py chainrows.', '']
+    grades = {}
+    for pl, name, repo, pin, path, line, kind, ax in CHAIN:
+        if kind == 'mathlib':
+            t = (rdn('b704_probe_out_zeta.txt') or '').split(NL)
+            sha, st, k, e, sl = 'de5ce8a', NL.join(t[3:5]), 'def', None, []
+            where = 'Mathlib de5ce8a9 (SIDE-explicit-formula v0.37 = 38e2734`s lake-manifest.json :10) %s :%d -- the elaborated statement of relay data/b704_probe_out_zeta.txt :4-:5 (run 39721668586 at v0.37; no Mathlib clone at the pin)' % (path, line)
+        else:
+            got = _chain_read(repo, pin, path, line, kind)
+            if got is None:
+                L += ['--- (P3) :%d  %s  ### NOT READ AT %s %s:%d' % (pl, name, pin, path, line), '']
                 continue
-            l = lines[h['line'] - 1]
-            at = l.find(h['what']) if h['what'] in l else l.lower().find(h['what'].lower())
-            out.append((p, h['line'], h['kind'], h['what'], _full_sentence(l, max(at, 0)),
-                        PL.HAND.get((os.path.basename(p), h['line'], h['what'].lower()), 'NO HAND NOTE')))
-    return out
+            sha, st, k, e, sl = got
+            where = '%s %s = %s : %s :%d' % (os.path.basename(repo), pin, sha, path, line)
+        if k == 'field':
+            g, why = 'NOT GRADED', 'a structure field: the E0 rule grades a declaration`s statement, and a field is no declaration'
+        else:
+            short = name.split('.')[-1]
+            mm = re.search(r'\b' + re.escape(short) + r'\b', st)
+            try:
+                g, why, _b = E0.grade(st[mm.end():] if mm else st, 'def' if k in ('def', 'abbrev') else 'theorem')
+            except Exception as ex:   # noqa: BLE001
+                g, why = 'UNCLASSED', ' '.join(str(ex).split())[:300]
+        grades[g] = grades.get(g, 0) + 1
+        axt, axs = _chain_axioms(ax, sha if kind != 'mathlib' else '')
+        L += ['--- (P3) :%d  %s%s' % (pl, name, '  (NO HOMEBREW ROW)' if pl in _NOHB else ''), '    at   : ' + where]
+        L += ['    stmt : ' + s for s in st.split(NL)]
+        L += ['    kind %s ; GRADE %s ; %s' % (k, g, why), '    sorry in the span%s : %s' % (' :%d-:%d' % (line, e) if e else '', sl or 'NONE'),
+              '    axioms : %s ; %s' % (axt, axs), '']
+    L.append('### ### **DECLARATIONS %d ; GRADES %s ; AXIOMS READ AT THE PIN %d ; NOT READ AT THIS PIN %d ; SORRY IN A SPAN %d.**' % (
+        len(CHAIN), ', '.join('%s %d' % kv for kv in sorted(grades.items())),
+        sum(1 for l in L if l.startswith('    axioms : ') and 'not read at this pin' not in l),
+        sum(1 for l in L if l.startswith('    axioms : axioms not read at this pin')),
+        sum(1 for l in L if l.startswith('    sorry in the span') and not l.endswith('NONE'))))
+    put_txt('%s_chain_rows.txt' % ACT, L)
+    print('bank data/%s_chain_rows.txt' % ACT)
+    print(L[-1])

+# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
+"""b715_reg_gate.py -- THE REGISTRATION GATE, RUN BEFORE THE LOCK. ### ALL THREE ARMS, THE DRY-RUN STEP AND THE CLOSING'S DRY RUN.
+
+### ### **THE GATE MODULE IS IMPORTED, NEVER COPIED**, and its own fixtures are run here in both
+### polarities before its verdict is trusted -- `b347`'s bar-floor arms and `b359`'s straddle arm.
+### ### **AND THE RECORD IS WRITTEN BY THIS FILE AND NOT BY A SHELL PIPE**, because a shell pipe on this
+### machine prepends a UTF-8 BOM (`b298`, `b305`) and a later arm reading the record would meet a byte
+### the writer never wrote. ### Carried from b714_reg_gate.py by the Write tool, the act's names changed; the closing dry-run step as
+### b675's rules bank row 1 orders it (relay data/b675_rules.txt); the dry-run step's trail Defects line AND Resolved line read against the
+### defects bank inside the record tool's drycheck (the ferry's Component 5).
+"""
+import io
+import os
+import sys
+
+ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
+sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(ROOT, 'tools'))
+import registration_gate as RG   # noqa: E402
+
+D = os.path.join(ROOT, 'data')
+REG = os.path.join(D, 'b715_registration_2026-10-26.txt')
+OUT = os.path.join(D, 'b715_reg_gate.txt')
+
+if hasattr(sys.stdout, 'reconfigure'):
+    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+
+L = []
+
+
+def rec(s=''):
+    L.append(s)
+    print(s)
+
+
+def main():
+    rec('=' * 100)
+    rec('b715 -- THE REGISTRATION GATE, RUN BEFORE THE LOCK.')
+    rec('=' * 100)
+    rec('--- INDEX-QUERY GATE (b185) ---')
+    code, lines = RG.check(REG)
+    for ln in lines:
+        rec(ln)
+    ok_index = (code == 0)
+    rec('  ### index-query arm exit code : %d ### -- PASS only on `0`' % code)
+    txt = io.open(REG, encoding='utf-8').read()
+    bf = RG.bar_floor_self_test(False)
+    st = RG.straddle_self_test(False)
+    rec('')
+    rec('  bar-floor fixtures both polarities : %s ; straddle fixtures both polarities : %s' % (bf, st))
+    floor_misses, arm_misses, n_thr, n_multi = RG.bar_floor_check(txt)
+    misses, n_repo = RG.straddle_check(txt)
+    rec('  thresholds %d FLOOR MISSES %d %s' % (n_thr, len(floor_misses), floor_misses or []))
+    rec('  multi-arm  %d ARM MISSES   %d %s' % (n_multi, len(arm_misses), arm_misses or []))
+    rec('  arm+repo-state paragraphs %d UNDECLARED %d %s' % (n_repo, len(misses), misses or []))
+    import subprocess
+    rec('')
+    rec('--- THE DRY-RUN STEP ((R267)(2)): the entry and the trail record through their guards, the face`s needles, the trail`s Defects and Resolved lines ---')
+    dr = subprocess.run([sys.executable, '-X', 'utf8', os.path.join(ROOT, 'tools', 'b715_record.py'), 'drycheck', REG, 'standin'], capture_output=True,
+                        text=True, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+    for ln in ((dr.stdout or '') + (dr.stderr or '')).rstrip(chr(10)).split(chr(10)):
+        rec('  ' + ln)
+    dry_clean = dr.returncode == 0 and 'DRY-RUN STEP : CLEAN' in (dr.stdout or '')
+    rec('  ### ### **THE DRY-RUN STEP : %s** (exit %d)' % ('CLEAN' if dry_clean else 'NOT CLEAN', dr.returncode))
+    rec('')
+    rec('--- THE CLOSING DRY-RUN STEP (b675 rules row 1): the closing tool run dry, its readings ---')
+    cr = subprocess.run([sys.executable, '-X', 'utf8', os.path.join(ROOT, 'tools', 'b715_closing.py'), 'dry'], capture_output=True,
+                        text=True, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+    for ln in ((cr.stdout or '') + (cr.stderr or '')).rstrip(chr(10)).split(chr(10)):
+        rec('  ' + ln)
+    closing_clean = cr.returncode == 0 and 'THE CLOSING DRY-RUN : READ' in (cr.stdout or '')
+    rec('  ### ### **THE CLOSING DRY-RUN STEP : %s** (exit %d)' % ('CLEAN' if closing_clean else 'NOT CLEAN', cr.returncode))
+    clear = bool(ok_index) and bf and st and not floor_misses and not arm_misses and not misses and dry_clean and closing_clean
+    rec('  ### ### **GATE VERDICT : %s**' % ('CLEAR' if clear else 'NOT CLEAR'))
+    rec('=' * 100)
+    with io.open(OUT, 'w', encoding='utf-8', newline=chr(10)) as fh:
+        fh.write(chr(10).join(L) + chr(10))
+    print('  written: %s' % os.path.basename(OUT))
+    return 0 if clear else 1
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    sys.exit(main())

+
+def _need(x):
+    if not x:
+        raise ValueError('### the reader read nothing')
+    return x
+
+
+PLANTED = [
+    ('score row', 'read_score_row', ('N3', ['HELD', 'a planted score text'])),
+    ('trail head with a backtick possessive', 'read_head', '### b999 — a planted trail: the page`s probe read, the paper`s cut landed'),
+    ('entry title with a backtick possessive', 'read_head', '## The planted entry: the page`s nodes, the README`s line'),
+    ('branch row (the branches bank`s shape, a named local row deleted)', 'read_branch_row', dict(repo='D:/x', branch='push-b999-root', where='local',
+                                                                                                tip='0' * 40, merged=True, fate='DELETED')),
+    ('defect row (the defects bank`s shape, its continuation joined)', 'read_defect_row', ["    (a) THE SEAT'S: A PLANTED DEFECT. Its text,",
+                                                                                          '        wrapped.']),
+    ('runner row (the runner rows bank`s shape)', 'read_runner_row', dict(kernel='SIDE-x', label='run1', ref='0' * 40, run_id=1, gate='DERIVES')),
+    ('runner row with the gate NOT DERIVES (the port runs` shape)', 'read_runner_row', dict(kernel='SIDE-x', label='port', ref='0' * 40, run_id=1, gate='NOT DERIVES')),
+]
+
+
+def planted():
+    import tempfile
+    import b715_record as REC
+    out = []
+    for shape, reader, row in PLANTED:
+        fn = getattr(REC, reader)
+        try:
+            if reader == 'read_head':
+                with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='b715_spec_') as d:
+                    led = os.path.join(d, 'LEDGER.md')
+                    with open(led, 'wb') as fh:
+                        fh.write(b'# a scratch ledger\n')
+                    REC.R.append_to(led, '\n' + row + ': planted.\n')
+                    got = fn(led, row)
+            elif reader == 'read_score_row':
+                got = fn(*row)
+            elif reader == 'read_defect_row':
+                got = _need(fn(row, 0))
+            else:
+                got = fn(row)
+            out.append((shape, bool(got), repr(got)[:120]))
+        except Exception as e:
+            out.append((shape, False, '%s: %s' % (type(e).__name__, str(e)[:100])))
+    return out
+
+
+def count_arms(text):
+    return len(set(re.findall(r'\b[GF]-[A-Z0-9-]+', text)) - {'G-NO'})
+
+
+def carried(text):
+    m = re.search(r'THE ARMS FAILING THERE ON REAL STATE ARE DECLARED BEFORE THE LOCK, BY NAME:\*\* ([^.]*)\.', ' '.join(text.split()))
+    return len(set(re.findall(r'`([GF]-[A-Z0-9-]+)`', m.group(1)))) if m else 0
+
+
+def controls():
+    import b715_record as REC
+    ctl = []
+    for what, f in (('a score row of another shape', lambda: REC.read_score_row('X9', 'HELD')),
+                    ('a head the ledger does not carry', lambda: REC.read_head(os.path.join(ROOT, 'data', 'b715_ferry.txt'), '### b999 absent')),
+                    ('a branch row with no side', lambda: REC.read_branch_row(dict(repo='D:/x', branch='b', tip='0' * 40, fate='DELETED'))),
+                    ('a branch row with a fate of another name', lambda: REC.read_branch_row(dict(repo='D:/x', branch='b', where='local', tip='0' * 40,
+                                                                                                fate='GONE'))),
+                    ('a branch row with a short tip', lambda: REC.read_branch_row(dict(repo='D:/x', branch='b', where='remote', tip='0' * 7,
+                                                                                     fate='DELETED'))),
+                    ('a defect row with no letter', lambda: _need(REC.read_defect_row(['    THE SEAT`S: NO LETTER HERE.'], 0))),
+                    ('a runner row with a short ref', lambda: REC.read_runner_row(dict(kernel='SIDE-x', label='run1', ref='0' * 7, run_id=1,
+                                                                                       gate='DERIVES'))),
+                    ('a runner row with a gate of another name', lambda: REC.read_runner_row(dict(kernel='SIDE-x', label='run1', ref='0' * 40, run_id=1,
+                                                                                                  gate='MAYBE'))),
+                    ('a runner row with a dot in its label', lambda: REC.read_runner_row(dict(kernel='SIDE-x', label='prints-v0.17', ref='0' * 40, run_id=1,
+                                                                                              gate='DERIVES'))),
+                    ('the repaired defects reader called with no act', lambda: REC.R._defect_short())):
+        try:
+            f()
+            ctl.append((what, False))
+        except Exception:
+            ctl.append((what, True))
+    return ctl
+
+
+def _git(repo, *a):
+    r = subprocess.run(['git', '-C', repo] + list(a), capture_output=True, text=True, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+    return (r.stdout or '').strip()
+
+
+def refresh_commands(bank='b715_commands.json'):
+    """(R325)(3)(v), standing clauses v3: THE COMMAND SCREEN READS A FRESH BANK -- the command bank rewritten by the record tool's `commands`
+    route in this step, before the face's counts are read from it (b714's face read ANYPIPES 7 from a bank last written before its eighth
+    pipe, events :3). Returns the bank's `at` stamp after the refresh, or None where the refresh did not write it."""
+    p = os.path.join(ROOT, 'data', bank)
+    t0 = time.time()
+    r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, '-I', '-X', 'utf8', os.path.join(ROOT, 'tools', 'b715_record.py'), 'commands'], capture_output=True,
+                       text=True, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+    if r.returncode != 0 or not os.path.exists(p) or os.path.getmtime(p) < t0:
+        return None
+    try:
+        return json.load(io.open(p, encoding='utf-8')).get('at')
+    except Exception:
+        return None
+
+
+def main(argv):
+    print('=' * 100)
+    fresh = refresh_commands()
+    print('  ### (R325)(3)(v) THE COMMAND BANK REFRESHED IN THIS STEP: %s' % (fresh or '### NOT REFRESHED'))
+    if not fresh:
+        print('  ### REFUSING TO EMIT A SPEC FROM A COMMAND BANK NOT WRITTEN IN THIS STEP.')
+        return 4
+    print('b715_regspec.py -- THE SATISFIABILITY SPEC. ### THE COUNTER IS IMPORTED, NOT COPIED.')
+    print('=' * 100)
+    print('  counter source : %s' % os.path.basename(CNT.__file__))
+    if not CNT.self_test():
+        print('  ### REFUSING TO EMIT A SPEC FROM A COUNTER THAT FAILS ITS OWN FIXTURES.')
+        return 2
+    P = planted()
+    print('  ### (R263)(3) THE PLANTED ROWS, ONE PER SHAPE A SEALED ROUTE READS, EACH THROUGH THE RECORD TOOL`S READER:')
+    for shape, ok, what in P:
+        print('      %-58s %s -- %s' % (shape, 'READ' if ok else '### REFUSED', what))
+    print('  ### ### **PLANTED ROWS %d ; READ %d ; REFUSED %d.**' % (len(P), sum(1 for p in P if p[1]), sum(1 for p in P if not p[1])))
+    ctl = controls()
+    for what, ok in ctl:
+        print('      CONTROL %-60s %s' % (what, 'REFUSED, AS WANTED' if ok else '### READ -- THE READER IS VACUOUS'))
+    if not all(ok for _w, ok in ctl):
+        print('  ### REFUSING TO EMIT A SPEC: A READER TOOK A ROW OF ANOTHER SHAPE.')
+        return 4
+    if not all(p[1] for p in P):
+        print('  ### REFUSING TO EMIT A SPEC: A SEALED ROUTE`S READER FAILED ON A PLANTED ROW.')
+        return 4
+    text = io.open(REG, encoding='utf-8').read() if os.path.exists(REG) else ''
+    n, hits = CNT.count_predictions(text)
+    print()
+    print('  registration : %s' % os.path.basename(REG))
+    print('  bytes/lines  : %d / %d' % (len(text.encode('utf-8')), len(text.splitlines())))
+    print('  ### ARTIFACT-COUNT PREDICTIONS FOUND : %d' % n)
+    for ln, txt in hits:
+        print('      line %-4d  %s' % (ln, txt))
+
+    def jcount(name, f):
+        try:
+            return f(json.load(io.open(os.path.join(ROOT, 'data', name), encoding='utf-8')))
+        except Exception:
+            return 0
+
+    def tcount(name, pat):
+        try:
+            return len(re.findall(pat, io.open(os.path.join(ROOT, 'data', name), encoding='utf-8').read(), re.M))
+        except Exception:
+            return 0
+
+    def tfirst(name, pat, k=1):
+        try:
+            m = re.search(pat, io.open(os.path.join(ROOT, 'data', name), encoding='utf-8').read())
+            return int(m.group(k)) if m else 0
+        except Exception:
+            return 0
+    ef_tags_after = [t for t in _git(K.EF_DIR, 'tag', '--contains', K.EF_V037).split(chr(10)) if t.strip() and t.strip() != 'v0.37']
+    lv_tags_after = [t for t in _git(K.LV_DIR, 'tag', '--contains', K.PORT_TIP).split(chr(10)) if t.strip() and t.strip() != K.LV_TAG]
+    join_tags = [t for t in _git(K.JOIN_DIR, 'tag', '--list').split(chr(10)) if t.strip()] if os.path.isdir(K.JOIN_DIR) else []
+    port_commits = _git(K.LV_DIR, 'rev-list', '--count', '%s..refs/heads/%s' % (K.PORT_TIP, K.PORT_BRANCH))
+    lean_files = [f for f in _git(K.LV_DIR, 'diff', '--name-only', K.PORT_TIP, K.PORT_NEW_TIP).split(chr(10)) if f.strip().endswith('.lean')]
+    first_relay = (_git(ROOT, 'rev-list', '--reverse', '%s..HEAD' % K.PRE_RELAY).split(chr(10)) or [''])[0].strip()
+    measured = dict(ARMS=count_arms(text), READINGS=len(set(re.findall(r'\*\*READING \((\d+)\) --', text))),
+                    EXPECT=len(re.findall(r'\*\*\((?:N|S)\d\)\*\*', text)), BARS=len(re.findall(r'^### BAR \d+', text, re.M)),
+                    TESTS=jcount('b715_tests_stepzero.json', len), QUESTIONS=tcount('b715_author_answers.txt', r'^### PROMPT '),
+                    LINES=jcount('b715_record_lines.json', lambda j: len(j.get('lines') or [])),
+                    MISSES=tfirst('b715_citation_check.txt', r'(?<!ANSWER )CITES CHECKED \d+ ; OK \d+ ; MISSES (\d+)'),
+                    REPAIRS=tfirst('b715_standing_controls.txt', r'REPAIRS (\d+), EACH WITH BOTH INSTANCES True') + tcount('b715_hook_control.txt', r'THE REPAIR HOLDS True'),
+                    PRESEAL=tcount('b715_author_words_preseal.txt', r'^## \(\d+\) '),
+                    CARRIED=carried(text), DISPATCHES=jcount('b715_commands.json', lambda j: len(j.get('dispatches') or [])),
+                    RUNS=jcount('b715_dispatches.json', lambda j: len([r for r in j.get('rows') or [] if r.get('run_id')])),
+                    PORTCOMMITS=int(port_commits) if port_commits.isdigit() else 0, LEANFILES=len(lean_files),
+                    TAGSMADE=len(lv_tags_after) + len(ef_tags_after) + len([t for t in join_tags if t != K.JOIN_TAG[0]]),
+                    ORDERS=jcount('b715_order_lines.json', lambda j: len(j.get('lines') or [])),
+                    BINARIES=len([f for f in os.listdir(os.path.join(ROOT, 'data')) if re.match(r'^b715_\w+_hashes\.txt$', f)]),
+                    COMPUTE=len([n_ for n_ in ('import_closure', 'standing_scan', 'replay', 'toolset_coverage', 'standing_controls', 'hook_control', 'roster',
+                                               'pins_census', 'chain_rows', 'census_diff') if os.path.exists(os.path.join(ROOT, 'data', 'b715_%s.txt' % n_))]),
+                    SHAREDTOOLS=len(K.REPAIRED_TOOLS) + len(K.NEW_TOOLS),
+                    WHOLEREADS=len(re.findall(r"^    \([a-z]\) THE SEAT'S: THE CARRIED (?:SUITE|TOOLS?) READ BEYOND THE SPANS",
+                                              io.open(os.path.join(ROOT, 'data', 'b715_defects.txt'), encoding='utf-8').read(), re.M)),
+                    SEDS=jcount('b715_commands.json', lambda j: len(j.get('seds') or [])),
+                    REDIRECTS=jcount('b715_commands.json', lambda j: len(j.get('redirects') or [])),
+                    PIPES=jcount('b715_commands.json', lambda j: len(j.get('pipes') or [])),
+                    ANYPIPES=jcount('b715_commands.json', lambda j: len(j.get('any_pipes') or [])),
+                    PYPROGS=jcount('b715_commands.json', lambda j: len(j.get('py_programs') or [])),
+                    READOFF=tfirst('b715_read_calls.txt', r'OFFENCES (\d+) ; THE CAP`S PRECURSORS'))
+    print()
+    print('  ### ### **MEASURED OFF THE FACE, THE BANKS AND THE KERNELS, NOT TYPED:**')
+    for k in ('ARMS', 'READINGS', 'EXPECT', 'BARS', 'TESTS', 'QUESTIONS', 'PRESEAL', 'LINES', 'MISSES', 'REPAIRS', 'CARRIED', 'DISPATCHES', 'RUNS', 'PORTCOMMITS',
+              'LEANFILES', 'TAGSMADE', 'ORDERS', 'BINARIES', 'COMPUTE', 'SHAREDTOOLS', 'WHOLEREADS', 'SEDS', 'REDIRECTS',
+              'PIPES', 'ANYPIPES', 'PYPROGS', 'READOFF'):
+        print('      %-12s %d' % (k, measured[k]))
+    CLAUSES[:] = [(c, measured.get(cap, cap), measured.get(dem, dem), u, f) for (c, cap, dem, u, f) in CLAUSES]
+    clauses = [{"clause": c, "cap": cap, "demand": (n if (dem is None and c.startswith('artifact counts')) else (cap if dem is None else dem)),
+                "units": u, "from": frm} for (c, cap, dem, u, frm) in CLAUSES]
+    spec = {"registration": ("data/b715_registration_2026-10-26.txt -- b715, THE TOOLSET ACT: b714 AT ITS WEIGHT; THE CORRIGENDUM; ONE STANDING "
+                             "CLOSE TOOLSET AND ITS REPLAY; THE CLASS REPAIRS; THE MIRROR'S ROSTER; THE PINS CENSUS; THE CHAIN'S CENSUS ROWS, UNDER (R325)"),
+            "clauses": clauses}
+    d = (json.dumps(spec, indent=1, ensure_ascii=False) + chr(10)).encode('utf-8')
+    with open(SPEC + '.tmp', 'wb') as fh:
+        fh.write(d)
+    os.replace(SPEC + '.tmp', SPEC)
+    print()
+    print('  clauses emitted : %d' % len(clauses))
+    nz = [c for c in clauses if c['demand']]
+    print('  ### ### **CLAUSES WITH A NON-ZERO DEMAND : %d**' % len(nz))
+    print('  written : %s' % os.path.basename(SPEC))
+    print('=' * 100)
+    return 0
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1:]))

+"""b715 -- THE STANDING REPORT TOOL, (R325)(3): data/b715_report.txt (.json). A REPORT, NOT AN ARM.
+
+Carried from b714's standing report (tools/b714_report.py) by diff at Component 6 and its lines rewritten by the Edit tool: b714's lines
+(the lv port's tag, pair 7's rectangle, the merge, the law, the residual, the Sonin price, the pilot) read banks this act does not write,
+and are not carried; the lines are b715's: the census rows at their pins (KERNEL), none certified (CERTIFIED), the standing toolset and its
+replay, the class repairs, the roster from main and the pins census (REGISTERED), and what the act holds (HELD).
+Three routes:
+  bank    (before the root) each line's figures recomputed from the act's banks, beside each its SOURCE -- the bank's path, the row cited by
+          line and the bank's sha256 now -- the lines printed in footing order (KERNEL, CERTIFIED, REGISTERED, HELD), a footing no line stands
+          on printed as NONE; written through the membrane to data/b715_report.txt and data/b715_report.json. Nothing else is written.
+  dry     the lines printed, nothing written.
+  scores  (after the root) the scores line: all eight from data/b715_scores_close.json, each HELD or BROKEN, and the root line from
+          data/act_roots.txt; PRINTED ONLY -- no bank is written after the root (N4), and the membrane refuses one.
+The tool decides nothing: no verdict is gated on its lines (no arm reads its exit code), and a line it cannot recompute prints as such.
+"""
+import hashlib
+import io
+import json
+import os
+import re
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import time
+
+T = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
+ROOT = os.path.dirname(T)
+D = os.path.join(ROOT, 'data')
+sys.path.insert(0, T)
+import b715_worklist as K          # noqa: E402
+
+NL = '\n'
+ACT = 'b715'
+FOOTINGS = ('KERNEL', 'CERTIFIED', 'REGISTERED', 'HELD')
+PP = K.PP
+if hasattr(sys.stdout, 'reconfigure'):
+    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
+
+
+def g(repo, *a):
+    r = subprocess.run(['git', '-C', repo] + list(a), capture_output=True)
+    return r.stdout.decode('utf-8', 'replace')
+
+
+def rd(name):
+    p = os.path.join(D, name)
+    return io.open(p, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').read().replace('\r', '') if os.path.exists(p) else ''
+
+
+def jl(name):
+    t = rd(name)
+    return json.loads(t) if t else {}
+
+
+def sha(name):
+    p = os.path.join(D, name)
+    return hashlib.sha256(open(p, 'rb').read()).hexdigest() if os.path.exists(p) else None
+
+
+def line_of(name, needle):
+    for i, l in enumerate(rd(name).split(NL), 1):
+        if needle in l:
+            return i
+    return None
+
+
+def seat(name, needle):
+    """the line's SOURCE: the bank's path, the row cited by line (the first line carrying the needle), its sha256 now."""
+    ln, now = line_of(name, needle), sha(name)
+    return dict(bank='data/' + name, row=ln, sha256=now, ok=bool(now) and ln is not None,
+                text='relay data/%s :%s ; sha256 %s' % (name, ln if ln is not None else '### NEEDLE NOT FOUND', (now or '### ABSENT')[:16]))
+
+
+def lines():
+    import b715_record as REC
+    f = REC._figs()
+    keys, seats = {}, []
+    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ KERNEL: THE CHAIN'S ROWS AT THEIR PINS ((R325)(7))
+    sc = seat('%s_chain_rows.txt' % ACT, '### ### **DECLARATIONS')
+    keys['ROWS'] = ('KERNEL -- THE CENSUS ROWS AT THEIR PINS: the reduction chain`s %s declarations each read at its pin by git show and graded '
+                    'under the E0 gate -- DERIVES %s, INTERFACES %s, not graded %s -- %d of them written to the census v0.8.2 on PLACE-papers main; '
+                    'axioms read at the pin %s, not read at this pin %s; one proved by `sorry` in its span. A reading of statements at pins, no '
+                    'build on this host. SOURCE %s.' % (f['cr_n'], f['cr_der'], f['cr_int'], int(f['cr_def']) + int(f['cr_ng']), f['cr_reached'],
+                                                         f['cr_ax'], f['cr_noax'], sc['text']))
+    seats.append(sc)
+    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ CERTIFIED: NONE THIS ACT
+    keys['CERT'] = ('CERTIFIED -- NONE this act: no certified count computed; pair 7`s rectangle certification carried to b716 ((R325)(8)), '
+                    'as b714`s banks state it.')
+    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ REGISTERED: THE STANDING TOOLSET AND ITS REPLAY ((R325)(3))
+    st = seat('%s_replay.txt' % ACT, '### ### **OBJECTS')
+    keys['TOOLSET'] = ('REGISTERED -- THE STANDING CLOSE TOOLSET AT %s: %s files under tools/standing/, %s act literals in the code, the act read '
+                       'from data/act_current.json; the replay over b714 and b713 %s of %s objects EQUAL, %s DIFFERS named, %s findings the old '
+                       'tools`; the coverage table %s STANDING of %s steps. SOURCE %s.' % (
+                           f['ver'], f['sc_files'], f['sc_lit'], f['rp_eq'], f['rp_obj'], f['rp_diff'], f['rp_find'], f['cv_std'], f['cv_steps'],
+                           st['text']))
+    seats.append(st)
+    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ REGISTERED: THE CLASS REPAIRS ((R325)(4))
+    sk = seat('%s_standing_controls.txt' % ACT, '### ### **CASES')
+    keys['REPAIRS'] = ('REGISTERED -- THE CLASS REPAIRS, %d WITH TWO INSTANCES EACH: %s cases as wanted %s for (i), (ii), (iv)-(vi); the hook`s '
+                       '%s of %s for (iii), its repair holding %s. SOURCE %s.' % (
+                           f['n_repairs'], f['cc_cases'], f['cc_wanted'], f['hc_wanted'], f['hc_cases'], f['hc_holds'], sk['text']))
+    seats.append(sk)
+    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ REGISTERED: THE ROSTER FROM MAIN ((R325)(5))
+    sr = seat('%s_roster.txt' % ACT, '### ### **ROSTER CONTROLS')
+    keys['ROSTER'] = ('REGISTERED -- THE MIRROR`S ROSTER FROM MAIN: the b707 roster`s families read from PLACE-papers main, %s joining, controls %s; '
+                      'the builder reads it at build time. SOURCE %s.' % (', '.join(f['ro_joins']), f['ro_ctl'], sr['text']))
+    seats.append(sr)
+    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ REGISTERED: THE PINS CENSUS ((R325)(6))
+    sp = seat('%s_pins_census.txt' % ACT, '### ### **PINS ROSTER CONTROLS')
+    keys['PINS'] = ('REGISTERED -- THE PINS CENSUS AT %s REPOSITORIES: read from %s texts on main, %s hard failures named and carried, controls %s. '
+                    'SOURCE %s.' % (f['pins_n'], f['pins_texts'], f['pins_hard'], f['pins_ctl'], sp['text']))
+    seats.append(sp)
+    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ HELD
+    sh = seat('%s_ferry.txt' % ACT, '(11) THE DRAFT stands at v5, HELD')
+    keys['HELD'] = ('HELD -- the draft at v5 (%s), unpublished; no bench number in a keystone; the items of (R325)(8) carried to b716 from their '
+                    'banks, none acted on here: the heads draft and its footings question, the six lint hits, the 19 names with no pin, the '
+                    'SIDESystem finding, the composition law and its -39 registration, the residual, the rectangle, the Sonin price and the law`s '
+                    'identity reading. SOURCE %s.' % (K.DRAFT, sh['text']))
+    seats.append(sh)
+    order = [('KERNEL', ['ROWS']), ('CERTIFIED', ['CERT']), ('REGISTERED', ['TOOLSET', 'REPAIRS', 'ROSTER', 'PINS']), ('HELD', ['HELD'])]
+    out = []
+    for ft, ks in order:
+        out += [keys[k] for k in ks]
+    return out, keys, seats, order
+
+
+def bank():
+    import act_membrane as MEM
+    import b715_record as REC
+    out, keys, seats, order = lines()
+    bad = [s['bank'] for s in seats if not s['ok']]
+    at = time.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ', time.gmtime())
+    L = ['%s -- THE STANDING REPORT, (R325)(3): THE LINES IN FOOTING ORDER, EACH RECOMPUTED FROM ITS BANKS (%s)' % (ACT, at), '',
+         '### rows are the report`s lines in footing order (KERNEL, CERTIFIED, REGISTERED, HELD), each line`s figures recomputed from the act`s',
+         '### banks, its SOURCE the bank by path, row and sha256. A report, not an arm: no verdict reads it. The scores line is printed after the',
+         '### root by `tools/b715_report.py scores`, and written nowhere.', '']
+    L += ['  (%d) %s' % (i + 1, l) for i, l in enumerate(out)]
+    L += ['', '### SOURCES NOT SEATED (a needle not found or a bank absent): %s' % (bad or 'NONE'),
+          '### ### **THE STANDING REPORT: %d LINES IN FOOTING ORDER ; SOURCES %d, SEATED %d ; THE SCORES LINE AFTER THE ROOT.**' % (
+              len(out), len(seats), len(seats) - len(bad))]
+    J = dict(at=at, lines=out, keys=keys, seats=seats, unseated=bad, footing=dict(order))
+    for name, b in (('%s_report.txt' % ACT, (NL.join(L) + NL).encode('utf-8')),
+                    ('%s_report.json' % ACT, (json.dumps(J, indent=1, ensure_ascii=False) + NL).encode('utf-8'))):
+        p = os.path.join(D, name)
+        MEM.guard_path(ACT, p, REC.R.ROOT_EXCLUDE)
+        with open(p + '.tmp', 'wb') as fh:
+            fh.write(b)
+        os.replace(p + '.tmp', p)
+    print(NL.join(L[6:]))
+
+
+def dry():
+    out, _k, seats, _o = lines()
+    print(NL.join('  (%d) %s' % (i + 1, l) for i, l in enumerate(out)))
+    print('### SOURCES NOT SEATED: %s' % ([s['bank'] for s in seats if not s['ok']] or 'NONE'))
+
+
+def scores():
+    sc = jl('%s_scores_close.json' % ACT)
+    keys = ('N1', 'N2', 'N3', 'N4', 'N5', 'S1', 'S2', 'S3')
+    word = dict(HELD='HELD', REFUTED='BROKEN')
+    roots = [l for l in rd('act_roots.txt').split(NL) if l.startswith(ACT + ' ')]
+    print('### THE ROOT: %s' % (roots[-1] if roots else '### NOT APPENDED -- the scores line waits on the root'))
+    print('### THE SCORES (relay data/%s_scores_close.json, sha256 %s…): %s' % (ACT, (sha('%s_scores_close.json' % ACT) or '### ABSENT')[:16],
+          ' ; '.join('%s %s' % (k, word.get((sc.get(k) or ['?'])[0], (sc.get(k) or ['?'])[0])) for k in keys)))
+    print('### ### **SCORES %d OF %d HELD -- PRINTED AFTER THE ROOT, WRITTEN NOWHERE.**' % (sum(1 for k in keys if (sc.get(k) or [''])[0] == 'HELD'), len(keys)))
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    r = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else ''
+    if r not in ('bank', 'dry', 'scores'):
+        print('usage: b715_report.py bank | dry | scores')
+        sys.exit(2)
+    dict(bank=bank, dry=dry, scores=scores)[r]()
